<h2>Method and limits</h2><p class='small'>Matching, sums and date comparisons are done in code. Where an investigation ran, a language model looked for an innocent explanation in the file; an item is marked explained only on three agreeing passes, a second review and specific citations. The tool sees only the documents in the folder. Compensation is compared with a configurable rule of thumb, not with the law of any jurisdiction.</p></body></html>

==> ./verify.py <==
"""One command to check that a change has not broken anything. Needs no model.

    python verify.py

Run it before every commit. If it prints PASS, the tests pass, the demo matter still raises
exactly the labelled findings, and the clean control file still raises nothing.
"""
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
STEPS = [
    ("regenerate the demo matter", [sys.executable, "make_demo.py"]),
    ("unit tests", [sys.executable, "-m", "unittest", "discover", "tests"]),
    ("stage 1 eval against ground truth", [sys.executable, "evals/run_eval.py"]),
    ("README results are current", [sys.executable, "evals/make_table.py", "--check"]),
    ("reports build", [sys.executable, "-m", "reconciler", "analyze", "demo_matter", "--out", "out"]),
]

for name, cmd in STEPS:
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True)
    print(f"{'ok  ' if result.returncode == 0 else 'FAIL'}  {name}")
    if result.returncode != 0:
        print(result.stdout[-3000:], result.stderr[-3000:], sep="\n")
        sys.exit(f"\nFAIL at: {name}")
print("\nPASS")

<p class='note'>This draft was produced before the points were checked for ordinary explanations in the documents themselves. Some may drop away once that is done.</p>
<h2>What this does and does not mean</h2>
<p>The amount in question is the total of the individual discrepancies. It is not a calculation of loss or of what anyone owes. Some items may be explained once documents are produced.</p><p>These are questions, not conclusions. A difference between two documents can have an ordinary explanation, and nothing here says that anyone acted improperly. The next step is for your lawyer to ask the executor for the documents and explanations listed in the request letter.</p>

<h2>Summary by category</h2>

<h2>Method and limits</h2><p class='small'>Matching, sums and date comparisons are done in code. Where an investigation ran, a language model looked for an innocent explanation in the file; an item is marked explained only on three agreeing passes, a second review and specific citations. The tool sees only the documents in the folder. Compensation is compared with a configurable rule of thumb, not with the law of any jurisdiction.</p></body></html>

==> ./reconciler/extract.py <==
"""Turn narrative documents into checkable statements.

This layer is rule-based on purpose: it is fast, free, reproducible, and gives the evals a
fixed baseline. An LLM extractor can be dropped in behind the same `statements()` interface
and scored against the same ground truth (see README, "Roadmap").
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Iterator, Optional

from .models import Evidence, TextDoc

# Document types we know how to recognise, both as produced documents and as references in letters.
DOC_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[str, re.Pattern]] = {
    "will": ("the Will", re.compile(r"\blast will\b|\bcopy of the will\b", re.I)),
    "t3_return": ("T3 trust returns", re.compile(r"\bT3\b|\btrust (income tax )?returns?\b", re.I)),
    "notice_of_assessment": ("CRA Notice of Assessment", re.compile(r"\bnotices? of assessment\b", re.I)),
    "clearance_application": ("application for a clearance certificate",
                              re.compile(r"\bapplication for (a )?clearance certificate\b", re.I)),
    "notice_of_reassessment": ("CRA Notice of Reassessment", re.compile(r"\bnotices? of reassessment\b", re.I)),
    "clearance_certificate": ("CRA Clearance Certificate", re.compile(r"\bclearance certificate\b", re.I)),
    "inventory": ("inventory of estate assets", re.compile(r"\binventory\b", re.I)),
    "appraisal": ("property appraisal", re.compile(r"\bappraisal\b", re.I)),
    "statement_of_accounts": ("statement of accounts", re.compile(r"\bstatement of accounts\b", re.I)),
    "legal_invoice": ("legal invoice", re.compile(r"\binvoice\b", re.I)),
    "bank_records": ("bank records", re.compile(r"\bbank (statements?|records)\b", re.I)),
}

# "We are giving / will give you this document."
PROMISE = re.compile(r"\benclos|\battach|\bwill provide\b|\bwill be provided\b|\bwill forward\b|\bto follow\b", re.I)
# "We do not have this document yet."
PENDING = re.compile(
    r"\bstill waiting\b|\bawaiting\b|\bnot yet (been )?received\b|"
    r"\b(has|have) not (yet )?(been )?received\b", re.I)

MONEY = re.compile(r"\$\s?([0-9][0-9,]*(?:\.[0-9]{2})?)")
SENTENCE_END = re.compile(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+")
STOP = set("the and for from with that this was were has have had its into net per via".split())


def tokens(text: str) -> set[str]:
    return {t for t in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", text.lower()) if len(t) >= 3 and t not in STOP}


def classify_doc(name: str, lines: list[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    """Classify a produced document from its file name and its title line only.
    (Letters mention many document types in their body; that must not count as producing them.)"""
    first = next((ln for ln in lines if ln.strip()), "")
    probe = name.replace("_", " ").replace("-", " ") + " " + first
    # A Notice of Assessment for a T3 return mentions both types; the one named first wins.
    hits = [(m.start(), key) for key, (_, p) in DOC_TYPES.items() if (m := p.search(probe))]
    return min(hits)[1] if hits else None


@dataclass
class Statement:
    doc: TextDoc
    line_no: int
    text: str

    def cite(self) -> Evidence:
        return Evidence(self.doc.name, f"line {self.line_no}", self.text)

    def doc_types(self) -> list[str]:
        return [k for k, (_, p) in DOC_TYPES.items() if p.search(self.text)]

    def amounts(self) -> list[Decimal]:
        return [Decimal(m.replace(",", "")) for m in MONEY.findall(self.text)]


def statements(doc: TextDoc) -> Iterator[Statement]:
    """Sentences of a narrative document, each tagged with its source line."""
    for i, line in enumerate(doc.lines, start=1):
        for sentence in SENTENCE_END.split(line.strip()):
            if sentence:
                yield Statement(doc, i, sentence)

==> ./reconciler/checks.py <==
"""The check registry.

Each check lives in its own file under reconciler/rules/ and registers itself with @check.
Any file in that folder whose name does not start with "_" is loaded automatically, so
adding a check never means editing this file. See AGENTS.md for the recipe.

Anything involving arithmetic or matching is done in plain code, never by a language model.
A finding is a discrepancy to follow up on, not a conclusion about anyone's conduct.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import pkgutil
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Callable

from .models import Finding, Matter


@dataclass
class Config:
    exact_window_days: int = 5        # same amount, dates this close: treated as the same item
    similar_window_days: int = 10     # similar payee, dates this close, different amount: mismatch
    late_window_days: int = 60        # same amount but dates this far apart: date discrepancy
    materiality: Decimal = Decimal("25.00")       # bank items below this are not flagged
    letter_amount_floor: Decimal = Decimal("1000")
    # Rule of thumb for executor compensation: a percentage of receipts plus a percentage of
    # disbursements. 2.5% + 2.5% is the customary Ontario starting point; set per jurisdiction.
    comp_rate_receipts: Decimal = Decimal("0.025")
    comp_rate_disbursements: Decimal = Decimal("0.025")
    form_signature_gap_days: int = 60    # form dated this long before it was signed: flag
    clearance_followup_days: int = 90    # final assessment to clearance application: flag beyond this


CheckFn = Callable[[Matter, Config], list[Finding]]
REGISTRY: list[CheckFn] = []
SEVERITY_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}


def check(fn: CheckFn) -> CheckFn:
    """Decorator: register a function (matter, config) -> list of Findings."""
    REGISTRY.append(fn)
    return fn


def run_all(m: Matter, cfg: Config | None = None) -> list[Finding]:
    cfg = cfg or Config()
    findings = [f for fn in REGISTRY for f in fn(m, cfg)]
    return sorted(findings, key=lambda f: (SEVERITY_ORDER[f.severity], f.type, f.key))


def _load_rules() -> None:
    from . import rules
    for mod in sorted(pkgutil.iter_modules(rules.__path__), key=lambda x: x.name):
        if not mod.name.startswith("_"):
            importlib.import_module(f"{rules.__name__}.{mod.name}")


_load_rules()

==> ./reconciler/retrieval.py <==
"""Ask questions of the matter. BM25 retrieval over every line, ledger row and finding,
with an optional language model that may only answer from the retrieved passages."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass

from . import llm
from .models import Finding, Matter


@dataclass
class Chunk:
    doc: str
    locator: str
    text: str


def _tok(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", text.lower())


def build_chunks(m: Matter, findings: list[Finding]) -> list[Chunk]:
    chunks = [Chunk(t.doc, t.locator, f"{t.date} {t.description} ${t.amount:,.2f} "
                    f"({'receipt' if t.direction == 'in' else 'disbursement'})")
              for t in m.accounts + m.bank]
    for key, (_, ev) in m.summary.items():
        chunks.append(Chunk(ev.doc, ev.locator, ev.quote))
    for d in m.texts:
        chunks += [Chunk(d.name, f"line {i}", ln.strip())
                   for i, ln in enumerate(d.lines, start=1) if ln.strip()]
    for f in findings:
        chunks.append(Chunk("findings", f"{f.type}:{f.key}", f"{f.title}. {f.detail}"))
    return chunks


class BM25:
    def __init__(self, chunks: list[Chunk], k1: float = 1.5, b: float = 0.75):
        self.chunks, self.k1, self.b = chunks, k1, b
        self.tf = [Counter(_tok(c.text)) for c in chunks]
        self.len = [sum(tf.values()) for tf in self.tf]
        self.avg = sum(self.len) / max(len(self.len), 1)
        df = Counter(term for tf in self.tf for term in tf)
        n = len(chunks)
        self.idf = {t: math.log(1 + (n - d + 0.5) / (d + 0.5)) for t, d in df.items()}

    def search(self, query: str, k: int = 6) -> list[Chunk]:
        q = _tok(query)
        scores = []
        for i, tf in enumerate(self.tf):
            s = 0.0
            for t in q:
                if t in tf:
                    f = tf[t]
                    s += self.idf[t] * f * (self.k1 + 1) / (
                        f + self.k1 * (1 - self.b + self.b * self.len[i] / self.avg))
            if s > 0:
                scores.append((s, i))
        return [self.chunks[i] for _, i in sorted(scores, reverse=True)[:k]]


SYSTEM = (
    "You answer questions about an estate file for a lawyer. Use ONLY the numbered passages. "
    "Cite every factual sentence with its passage number in square brackets, e.g. [2]. "
    "If the passages do not answer the question, say so. Do not characterise anyone's conduct "
    "or give a legal conclusion; describe what the documents show.")


def ask(m: Matter, findings: list[Finding], question: str, k: int = 6) -> dict:
    hits = BM25(build_chunks(m, findings)).search(question, k)
    passages = "\n".join(f"[{i}] ({c.doc}, {c.locator}) {c.text}" for i, c in enumerate(hits, start=1))
    answer = None
    if llm.configured() and hits:
        answer = llm.chat(SYSTEM, f"Passages:\n{passages}\n\nQuestion: {question}")
    return {"question": question, "answer": answer, "passages": hits}

==> ./reconciler/letter.py <==
"""Draft a document-request letter from the findings that survived investigation.

Template-based on purpose: the wording of a demand is the lawyer's call, and a draft built
from fixed language cannot introduce a fact that is not in the findings. Nothing is sent.
The wording for each finding type lives in catalog.py.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from .catalog import info
from .models import Finding


def draft_request(findings: list[Finding], investigations: list) -> str:
    verdict = {(i.type, i.key): i.verdict for i in investigations}
    keep = [f for f in findings if verdict.get((f.type, f.key), "stands") != "explained"]
    lines = [
        "> DRAFT FOR LAWYER REVIEW. NOT SENT. Check every item against its source before use.",
        "",
        "Re: Request for documents and information",
        "",
        "We have reviewed the statement of accounts, the bank record and the correspondence "
        "produced to date. We ask for the following:",
        "",
    ]
    for n, f in enumerate(keep, start=1):
        sources = "; ".join(f"{e.doc}, {e.locator}" for e in f.evidence)
        flag = "  [reviewer: investigation was inconclusive]" if verdict.get((f.type, f.key)) == "needs_human" else ""
        lines += [f"{n}. {f.title}. {info(f.type).ask}{flag}",
                  f"   (Source: {sources})", ""]
    lines.append("We would be grateful for a response within 30 days.")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

==> ./reconciler/catalog.py <==
"""One entry per finding type. The reports, the totals and the request letter all read from here.

Adding a check means adding its type here; a test fails if a check raises a type that is
missing from this table.

`counted` decides whether a finding's amount is added to the report's "amount in question".
It is False where adding it would count the same dollars twice (a wrong total is a symptom
of the items behind it) or where no money is in doubt (a payment made on a different date).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TypeInfo:
    label: str       # short name for tables
    category: str    # heading the finding is grouped under in the reports
    counted: bool    # add its amount to the total?
    ask: str         # what the request letter asks for


LEDGER = "Accounts and bank records disagree"
TOTALS = "Totals and balances (not added: these overlap with the items above)"
LETTERS = "Letters disagree with the records"
COMP = "Executor compensation"
WILL = "Gifts in the will not shown as paid"
COSTS = "Costs charged to the estate that may be avoidable"
TAX = "Tax payments without the notice behind them"
FEES = "Legal fees charged to the estate"
DOCS = "Documents and statements about them"
TIMING = "Timing (no amount in doubt)"

# The order here is the order categories appear in the reports.
CATALOG: dict[str, TypeInfo] = {
    "unsupported_entry": TypeInfo(
        "Payment in the accounts, not at the bank", LEDGER, True,
        "Please produce the voucher for this item and identify the account from which it was paid."),
    "undisclosed_transaction": TypeInfo(
        "Bank transaction missing from the accounts", LEDGER, True,
        "Please identify the payee and purpose of this transaction and explain why it does not appear in the accounts."),
    "amount_mismatch": TypeInfo(
        "Different amount in the accounts and at the bank", LEDGER, True,
        "Please explain the difference and produce the invoice and the cheque image or payment record."),
    "letter_vs_records": TypeInfo(
        "Figure in a letter differs from the records", LETTERS, True,
        "Please confirm which figure is correct and produce the supporting document."),
    "compensation_review": TypeInfo(
        "Compensation above the guideline", COMP, True,
        "Please provide the basis for the compensation claimed, including any record of time spent."),
    "agent_fee_offset": TypeInfo(
        "Agent fees paid as well as full compensation", COMP, False,   # overlaps with compensation_review
        "Please explain what administration the executor performed personally, and whether compensation "
        "has been adjusted for the fees paid to the agent."),
    "unpaid_bequest": TypeInfo(
        "Gift in the will with no payment shown", WILL, True,
        "Please confirm whether this gift has been paid and, if so, produce the record of payment."),
    "avoidable_cost": TypeInfo(
        "Penalty or late charge paid by the estate", COSTS, True,
        "Please explain how this charge arose and why it was paid from the estate."),
    "unsupported_tax_payment": TypeInfo(
        "Tax payment without the notice behind it", TAX, True,
        "Please produce the Notice of Reassessment to which this payment relates."),
    "invoice_arithmetic": TypeInfo(
        "Invoice does not add up", FEES, True,
        "Please provide a corrected invoice or the time records that support the fees billed."),
    "duplicate_time_entry": TypeInfo(
        "Time entry billed twice", FEES, True,
        "Please confirm whether this entry was billed twice and, if so, that the estate has been credited."),
    "personal_legal_fees": TypeInfo(
        "Possible personal advice billed to the estate", FEES, True,
        "Please explain why this work was charged to the estate rather than to the executor personally."),
    "missing_document": TypeInfo(
        "Document not produced", DOCS, False,
        "Please produce this document."),
    "timeline_contradiction": TypeInfo(
        "Letter contradicts a document's date", DOCS, False,
        "Please confirm the date on which this document was received, and by whom."),
    "billing_contradiction": TypeInfo(
        "Letter contradicts the lawyers' time records", DOCS, False,
        "Please confirm when this document was first received by the executor or her counsel."),
    "document_date_conflict": TypeInfo(
        "Form dated well before it was signed", DOCS, False,
        "Please confirm the date on which this form was signed and submitted."),
    "arithmetic_error": TypeInfo(
        "Total does not add up", TOTALS, False,
        "Please provide a corrected summary of accounts."),
    "balance_mismatch": TypeInfo(
        "Stated balance differs from the bank", TOTALS, False,
        "Please provide a reconciliation of the stated balance to the bank balance."),
    "date_mismatch": TypeInfo(
        "Different date in the accounts and at the bank", TIMING, False,
        "Please confirm the date on which this payment was made."),
    "clearance_delay": TypeInfo(
        "Delay between final assessment and clearance application", TIMING, False,
        "Please explain the interval between the final assessment and the application for the clearance certificate."),
}

FALLBACK = TypeInfo("Other", "Other", False, "Please provide an explanation.")


def info(finding_type: str) -> TypeInfo:
    return CATALOG.get(finding_type, FALLBACK)

==> ./reconciler/invoices.py <==
"""Read a lawyer's invoice (a text document) into time entries and stated totals.

Expected shape, one time entry per line, then the totals:

    2023-04-04 | JW | Review and file probate application | 1.50 | 450.00 | 675.00
    Fees: $13,000.00
    HST (13%): $1,690.00
    Total due: $14,690.00

Amounts on time entries are before tax. A negative entry is a credit.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional

from .models import Evidence, Matter, TextDoc

ENTRY = re.compile(r"^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\s*\|\s*(\w+)\s*\|\s*(.+?)\s*\|\s*(-?[\d.]+)\s*\|\s*([\d.,]+)\s*\|\s*(-?[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s*$")
MONEY = re.compile(r"\$\s?([0-9][0-9,]*\.[0-9]{2})")
LABELS = {"fees:": "fees", "hst": "tax", "total due:": "total"}


@dataclass
class TimeEntry:
    date: date
    timekeeper: str
    description: str
    hours: Decimal
    rate: Decimal
    amount: Decimal
    evidence: Evidence


@dataclass
class Invoice:
    doc: TextDoc
    entries: list[TimeEntry] = field(default_factory=list)
    stated: dict[str, tuple[Decimal, Evidence]] = field(default_factory=dict)   # fees, tax, total

    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        return self.stated[key][0] if key in self.stated else None


def _num(s: str) -> Decimal:
    return Decimal(s.replace(",", ""))


def parse_invoice(doc: TextDoc) -> Invoice:
    inv = Invoice(doc)
    for i, line in enumerate(doc.lines, start=1):
        ev = Evidence(doc.name, f"line {i}", line.strip())
        hit = ENTRY.match(line.strip())
        if hit:
            d, who, desc, hours, rate, amount = hit.groups()
            inv.entries.append(TimeEntry(date.fromisoformat(d), who, desc, _num(hours), _num(rate), _num(amount), ev))
            continue
        for label, key in LABELS.items():
            if line.strip().lower().startswith(label) and MONEY.search(line):
                inv.stated[key] = (_num(MONEY.search(line).group(1)), ev)
    return inv


def invoices(m: Matter) -> list[Invoice]:
    return [parse_invoice(d) for d in m.texts if d.doc_type == "legal_invoice"]

==> ./reconciler/tools.py <==
"""The tools the investigator agent may use. All are read-only.

Every result carries a `ref` ("document :: location"). The toolbox remembers each ref it has
handed out, so the agent's final citations can be checked against what it was actually shown.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation

from .models import Finding, Matter
from .retrieval import BM25, build_chunks


def ref(doc: str, locator: str) -> str:
    return f"{doc} :: {locator}"


SPECS = [
    {"type": "function", "function": {
        "name": "search_documents",
        "description": "Full-text search over every document, ledger row and bank line in the file. "
                       "Returns the best-matching passages with their refs.",
        "parameters": {"type": "object", "required": ["query"], "properties": {
            "query": {"type": "string", "description": "Keywords, e.g. a payee, a document name, an address"}}}}},
    {"type": "function", "function": {
        "name": "find_transactions",
        "description": "List transactions from the executor's accounts or the bank record, filtered by "
                       "date range, amount range and/or text in the description. Use it to look for "
                       "split payments, reversals and timing differences.",
        "parameters": {"type": "object", "required": ["source"], "properties": {
            "source": {"type": "string", "enum": ["accounts", "bank"]},
            "date_from": {"type": "string", "description": "YYYY-MM-DD"},
            "date_to": {"type": "string", "description": "YYYY-MM-DD"},
            "min_amount": {"type": "number"},
            "max_amount": {"type": "number"},
            "text": {"type": "string", "description": "Case-insensitive text the description must contain"}}}}},
    {"type": "function", "function": {
        "name": "add_amounts",
        "description": "Add dollar amounts exactly. Always use this instead of adding in your head.",
        "parameters": {"type": "object", "required": ["amounts"], "properties": {
            "amounts": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "number"}}}}}},
    {"type": "function", "function": {
        "name": "submit_verdict",
        "description": "End the investigation. Call exactly once, when you have looked where an "
                       "explanation would be.",
        "parameters": {"type": "object", "required": ["verdict", "explanation", "citations"], "properties": {
            "verdict": {"type": "string", "enum": ["explained", "stands", "needs_human"]},
            "explanation": {"type": "string", "description": "Two or three sentences on what the documents show"},
            "citations": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"},
                          "description": "Refs exactly as returned by the tools"}}}}},
]


class Toolbox:
    def __init__(self, matter: Matter, finding: Finding | None = None):
        self.m = matter
        self.index = BM25(build_chunks(matter, []))     # other findings are deliberately not searchable
        self.seen: dict[str, str] = {}                  # ref -> text shown
        self.original: set[str] = set()                 # refs already in the finding's evidence
        if finding:
            for ev in finding.evidence:
                r = ref(ev.doc, ev.locator)
                self.seen[r] = ev.quote
                self.original.add(r)

    def call(self, name: str, args: dict):
        try:
            if name == "search_documents":
                return self._search(str(args["query"]))
            if name == "find_transactions":
                return self._find(**args)
            if name == "add_amounts":
                return {"total": f"{sum((Decimal(str(a)) for a in args['amounts']), Decimal(0)):.2f}"}
            return {"error": f"unknown tool {name}"}
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, InvalidOperation) as e:
            return {"error": f"bad arguments for {name}: {e}"}

    def _search(self, query: str) -> list[dict]:
        out = []
        for c in self.index.search(query, k=6):
            r = ref(c.doc, c.locator)
            self.seen[r] = c.text
            out.append({"ref": r, "text": c.text})
        return out

    def _find(self, source: str, date_from: str | None = None, date_to: str | None = None,
              min_amount: float | None = None, max_amount: float | None = None,
              text: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
        if source not in ("accounts", "bank"):
            raise ValueError("source must be 'accounts' or 'bank'")
        rows = self.m.accounts if source == "accounts" else self.m.bank
        lo = date.fromisoformat(date_from) if date_from else date.min
        hi = date.fromisoformat(date_to) if date_to else date.max
        out = []
        for t in rows:
            if not lo <= t.date <= hi:
                continue
            if min_amount is not None and t.amount < Decimal(str(min_amount)):
                continue
            if max_amount is not None and t.amount > Decimal(str(max_amount)):
                continue
            if text and text.lower() not in t.description.lower():
                continue
            r = ref(t.doc, t.locator)
            self.seen[r] = t.cite().quote
            out.append({"ref": r, "date": t.date.isoformat(), "description": t.description,
                        "amount": f"{t.amount:.2f}", "direction": "money in" if t.direction == "in" else "money out"})
        return out[:25]

==> ./reconciler/summary.py <==
"""The numbers in the reports: dollar totals and the balance bridge. Plain code, no model.

Two rules keep the headline figure defensible:

  1. Nothing is counted twice. A wrong total and a balance gap are symptoms of the individual
     items, so their amounts are shown but not added (catalog.CATALOG decides, per type).
  2. Anything the investigator found to be explained by the file is left out of the total.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal

from .catalog import CATALOG, info
from .checks import Config
from .models import Finding, Matter
from .rules._common import small_bank_items

ZERO = Decimal(0)
OPEN = ("stands", "needs_human", "not_investigated")


def statuses(findings: list[Finding], investigations: list | None) -> dict[tuple[str, str], str]:
    """Status of each finding: explained | stands | needs_human | not_investigated."""
    verdict = {(i.type, i.key): i.verdict for i in investigations or []}
    return {(f.type, f.key): verdict.get((f.type, f.key), "not_investigated") for f in findings}


def totals(findings: list[Finding], investigations: list | None = None) -> dict:
    status = statuses(findings, investigations)
    counted = lambda f: info(f.type).counted and f.amount is not None  # noqa: E731
    by_status = {s: {"count": 0, "amount": ZERO} for s in (*OPEN, "explained")}
    for f in findings:
        row = by_status[status[(f.type, f.key)]]
        row["count"] += 1
        if counted(f):
            row["amount"] += f.amount

    categories = []
    for cat in dict.fromkeys(t.category for t in CATALOG.values()):       # catalog order, no repeats
        items = [f for f in findings if info(f.type).category == cat and status[(f.type, f.key)] in OPEN]
        if items:
            categories.append({
                "category": cat, "count": len(items),
                "counted": any(info(f.type).counted for f in items),
                "amount": sum((f.amount for f in items if f.amount is not None), ZERO),
                "has_amount": any(f.amount is not None for f in items)})

    return {
        "investigated": bool(investigations),
        "open_count": sum(by_status[s]["count"] for s in OPEN),
        "amount_in_question": sum((by_status[s]["amount"] for s in OPEN), ZERO),
        "by_status": by_status,
        "categories": categories,
    }


def balance_bridge(m: Matter, findings: list[Finding], cfg: Config | None = None) -> dict | None:
    """Show that the individual findings add up to the gap between the stated balance and the bank.

    A residual of zero means every dollar of the gap is traced to a listed item. A residual
    that is not zero means something moved the balance that no check has identified."""
    if "balance_on_hand" not in m.summary or m.bank_closing is None:
        return None
    cfg = cfg or Config()
    stated = m.summary["balance_on_hand"][0]
    small = small_bank_items(m, cfg.materiality)
    gap = m.bank_closing - stated
    lines = [(f, f.balance_effect) for f in findings if f.balance_effect]
    traced = sum((e for _, e in lines), ZERO) - small
    return {"stated": stated, "bank": m.bank_closing, "gap": gap, "lines": lines,
            "small_items": small, "residual": gap - traced}

==> ./reconciler/__main__.py <==
"""Command line:

    python -m reconciler analyze <matter>        checks only, then the reports (no model needed)
    python -m reconciler investigate <matter>    checks, the agent on each finding, then the reports
    python -m reconciler report <matter>         rebuild the reports from a saved investigation
    python -m reconciler ask <matter> "question"

Reports are written to --out: report_lawyer.html, report_client.html, schedule.csv,
draft_request_letter.md, findings.json (and investigations.json after `investigate`).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from . import llm
from .agent import investigate_all
from .checks import run_all
from .ingest import load_matter
from .report import load_investigations, money, write_all, write_json
from .retrieval import ask
from .summary import totals


def main() -> None:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="reconciler")
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    for name, text in (("analyze", "run the checks and write the reports"),
                       ("investigate", "run the checks, investigate each finding, write the reports"),
                       ("report", "rebuild the reports, using investigations.json in --out if present")):
        a = sub.add_parser(name, help=text)
        a.add_argument("matter")
        a.add_argument("--out", default="out")
        a.add_argument("--title", help="name of the matter for the report headings (default: folder name)")
    q = sub.add_parser("ask", help="ask a question of the matter")
    q.add_argument("matter")
    q.add_argument("question")
    args = p.parse_args()

    matter = load_matter(args.matter)
    findings = run_all(matter)

    if args.cmd == "ask":
        result = ask(matter, findings, args.question)
        print(result["answer"] + "\n" if result["answer"]
              else "(No language model configured; showing the most relevant passages.)\n")
        for i, c in enumerate(result["passages"], start=1):
            print(f"[{i}] {c.doc}, {c.locator}: {c.text}")
        return

    out = Path(args.out)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    title = args.title or Path(args.matter).resolve().name
    invs = None

    if args.cmd == "investigate":
        if not llm.configured():
            sys.exit("investigate needs a model: set RECONCILER_LLM_BASE_URL and RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL (see README)")
        invs = investigate_all(
            findings, matter, llm.Client(), reviewer=llm.reviewer_client(),
            progress=lambda f, i: print(f"[{i.verdict:<11}] {f.type:<24} {f.key}" + (f"  ({i.note})" if i.note else "")))
        write_json(invs, out / "investigations.json")
        print()
    elif args.cmd == "report" and (out / "investigations.json").exists():
        invs = load_investigations(out / "investigations.json")
        print(f"using {out / 'investigations.json'}\n")
    else:
        for f in findings:
            amount = money(f.amount) if f.amount is not None else ""
            print(f"[{f.severity:<6}] {f.type:<24} {amount:>12}  {f.title}")
        print()

    t = totals(findings, invs)
    print(f"{t['open_count']} open items, {money(t['amount_in_question'])} in question"
          + ("" if invs else " (not yet investigated)"))
    for path in write_all(matter, findings, invs, out, title):
        print(f"  {path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==> ./reconciler/agent.py <==
"""The investigator: for one finding, look for an innocent explanation in the file.

The model decides where to look. Code decides what it is allowed to conclude:

  * a verdict of "explained" must cite at least one passage that was not already in the finding;
  * every citation must be a ref a tool actually returned during this investigation;
  * no verdict within the step limit, or any model error, becomes "needs_human";
  * findings that turn on legal judgment are routed to a person without asking the model;
  * an arithmetic error or balance gap can never be marked "explained";
  * clearing takes three agreeing runs and a second reviewer; keeping takes one run.

The errors are not symmetric. Sending an explained item to a lawyer costs a few minutes;
clearing a real one loses it. Every fallback therefore lands on "needs_human".
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field

from .models import Evidence, Finding, Matter
from .tools import SPECS, Toolbox, ref

AGENT_VERSION = "v7"   # bump whenever the prompt or a rule changes, so eval runs stay comparable

VERDICTS = {"explained", "stands", "needs_human"}
# Questions of judgment, not lookup: is the compensation reasonable; who should bear a penalty.
JUDGMENT_ONLY = {"compensation_review", "avoidable_cost", "personal_legal_fees", "agent_fee_offset"}
# Findings the file cannot explain away. A total that does not equal the sum of its entries is
# wrong whatever else is in the file; the model may identify a likely cause, but that is a lead,
# not a clearance. A balance gap can have an innocent cause (an uncashed cheque), but a
# reconciliation is for a person to verify. Added after a model cleared both by pointing at
# another problem of the same size.
NOT_CLEARABLE = {"arithmetic_error": "stands", "invoice_arithmetic": "stands", "balance_mismatch": "needs_human"}

SYSTEM = """You assist a lawyer acting for a beneficiary of an estate. A first-pass check has \
flagged a discrepancy between the executor's accounts, the bank record and the correspondence. \
Before the lawyer raises it with the other side, test whether the file already explains it.

Method:
1. Work out what would have to be true for the discrepancy to be innocent: a payment split \
across several transactions, an entry later reversed, a document that is in the file under a \
different name, or a timing difference.
2. Use the tools to look in the places where that explanation would be.
3. Call submit_verdict.

Verdicts:
- explained: specific passages or transactions in the file account for the WHOLE discrepancy. \
Amounts must reconcile exactly (use add_amounts). Cite the passages that explain it.
- stands: you looked where an explanation would be and found none.
- needs_human: the explanation is partial or ambiguous, or the question is one of judgment.

Rules:
- Clearing a real problem is the worst mistake you can make. If in doubt, do not choose explained.
- Finding a second problem of the same size is not an explanation. It is a link; report it and \
choose stands or needs_human.
- Cite only refs returned by the tools, copied exactly.
- Describe what the documents show. Do not speculate about anyone's motives."""

BUDGET = ("You have {n} steps. A tool call is one step. Two or three well-aimed searches that "
          "find nothing are enough to conclude 'stands'; do not keep rephrasing the same search.")
LAST_STEP = ("This is your last step. Call submit_verdict now. If you looked where an explanation "
             "would be and found none, the verdict is 'stands'. If you found something partial, "
             "'needs_human'.")

REVIEW_SYSTEM = """You are the second reviewer of a proposed clearance. Another reviewer says a \
discrepancy in an estate file is innocently explained by the cited evidence. You see only the \
discrepancy, the proposed explanation and the cited evidence. Decide whether that evidence, on \
its own, fully accounts for the discrepancy.

Reject if any of these is true:
- the evidence accounts for only part of the amount, or the amounts do not reconcile exactly;
- the evidence is a different document, year, payee or property from the one in question;
- a cited item is itself flagged as a problem and would STILL be a problem if the explanation \
were accepted. Two halves of one payment, or a transaction and its reversal, resolve each \
other and are acceptable. A missing transfer that happens to equal an error elsewhere is not;
- you would need to assume a fact that the evidence does not state.

If in doubt, reject. A rejection only sends the item to a lawyer."""

REVIEW_SPEC = [{"type": "function", "function": {
    "name": "submit_review",
    "description": "Give your decision on the proposed clearance.",
    "parameters": {"type": "object", "required": ["decision", "reason"], "properties": {
        "decision": {"type": "string", "enum": ["confirm", "reject"]},
        "reason": {"type": "string", "description": "One or two sentences"}}}}}]


@dataclass
class Investigation:
    type: str
    key: str
    verdict: str
    explanation: str
    citations: list[Evidence] = field(default_factory=list)
    rejected_citations: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    note: str = ""                       # set when code overrode or supplied the verdict
    trace: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)   # every tool call, for audit
    checks: dict = field(default_factory=dict)        # repeat runs and second review, if any

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


def _args(call: dict) -> dict:
    raw = call.get("function", {}).get("arguments", {})
    if isinstance(raw, str):
        try:
            raw = json.loads(raw or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return {}
    return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}


def _finalize(f: Finding, box: Toolbox, args: dict, trace: list[dict]) -> Investigation:
    verdict = args.get("verdict")
    cited = [c for c in args.get("citations") or [] if isinstance(c, str)]
    valid = [c for c in cited if c in box.seen]
    rejected = [c for c in cited if c not in box.seen]
    note = ""
    if verdict not in VERDICTS:
        verdict, note = "needs_human", "model returned an unrecognised verdict"
    elif verdict == "explained" and rejected:
        verdict, note = "needs_human", "model cited a source that no tool returned"
    elif verdict == "explained" and f.type in NOT_CLEARABLE:
        verdict = NOT_CLEARABLE[f.type]
        note = "model said 'explained'; this kind of finding cannot be cleared by the file, so its account is kept as a lead only"
    elif verdict == "explained" and not any(c not in box.original for c in valid):
        verdict, note = "needs_human", "model said 'explained' without citing any new evidence"
    evidence = [Evidence(*c.split(" :: ", 1), box.seen[c]) for c in valid]
    return Investigation(f.type, f.key, verdict, str(args.get("explanation", "")),
                         evidence, rejected, note, trace)


def investigate(f: Finding, matter: Matter, client, max_steps: int = 10, sample: int = 0) -> Investigation:
    """One investigation. `client` is anything with complete(messages, tools, **opts) -> message.
    sample 0 is the deterministic run; higher samples use some temperature and a fixed seed,
    so that repeat runs are a real second opinion and not a replay."""
    if f.type in JUDGMENT_ONLY:
        return Investigation(f.type, f.key, "needs_human", "",
                             note="routed to a lawyer by rule: this turns on judgment, not on the documents")

    def bail(note: str) -> Investigation:
        return Investigation(f.type, f.key, "needs_human", "", note=note, trace=trace)

    opts = {"temperature": 0.5, "seed": sample} if sample else {}
    box = Toolbox(matter, f)
    trace: list[dict] = []
    brief = {"type": f.type, "title": f.title, "detail": f.detail,
             "evidence": [{"ref": ref(e.doc, e.locator), "text": e.quote} for e in f.evidence]}
    messages = [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM},
                {"role": "user", "content": "Finding to investigate:\n" + json.dumps(brief, indent=2)
                 + "\n\n" + BUDGET.format(n=max_steps)}]
    nudged = False

    for step in range(max_steps):
        if step == max_steps - 1 and max_steps > 1:
            # Proving a negative has no natural stopping point, so the model is told when to stop.
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": LAST_STEP})
        try:
            msg = client.complete(messages, SPECS, **opts)
        except Exception as e:  # network, bad JSON, model without tool support
            return bail(f"model error: {e}")
        calls = msg.get("tool_calls") or []
        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": msg.get("content") or "", "tool_calls": calls}
                        if calls else {"role": "assistant", "content": msg.get("content") or ""})
        if not calls:
            if nudged:
                return bail("model did not submit a verdict")
            nudged = True
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": "Use a tool, or call submit_verdict to finish."})
            continue
        for call in calls:
            name, args = call.get("function", {}).get("name", ""), _args(call)
            if name == "submit_verdict":
                return _finalize(f, box, args, trace)
            result = box.call(name, args)
            trace.append({"tool": name, "args": args, "result": result})
            messages.append({"role": "tool", "tool_call_id": call.get("id", ""), "content": json.dumps(result)})
    return bail("step limit reached without a verdict")


def review_clearance(f: Finding, inv: Investigation, flagged: dict[str, list[str]], client) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Second key. A fresh call that sees only the finding, the claim and the cited evidence.
    `flagged` maps a ref to the titles of OTHER findings that rely on it: a fact computed in
    code, handed to the reviewer so it does not have to guess whether a citation is itself
    in dispute."""
    packet = {
        "discrepancy": {"title": f.title, "detail": f.detail,
                        "evidence": [{"ref": ref(e.doc, e.locator), "text": e.quote} for e in f.evidence]},
        "proposed_explanation": inv.explanation,
        "cited_evidence": [{"ref": ref(e.doc, e.locator), "text": e.quote,
                            "also_flagged_as": flagged.get(ref(e.doc, e.locator), [])}
                           for e in inv.citations],
    }
    try:
        msg = client.complete([{"role": "system", "content": REVIEW_SYSTEM},
                               {"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(packet, indent=2)}], REVIEW_SPEC)
    except Exception as e:
        return False, f"reviewer error: {e}"
    for call in msg.get("tool_calls") or []:
        if call.get("function", {}).get("name") == "submit_review":
            args = _args(call)
            if args.get("decision") in ("confirm", "reject"):
                return args["decision"] == "confirm", str(args.get("reason", ""))
    return False, "reviewer gave no usable answer"


def investigate_checked(f: Finding, matter: Matter, client, all_findings: list[Finding],
                        runs: int = 3, verify: bool = True, max_steps: int = 10, reviewer=None) -> Investigation:
    """Investigate, and make "explained" earn its place.

    A first run that keeps the finding is accepted as is: that is the safe direction. A first
    run that clears it must be matched by every repeat run and then confirmed by the second
    reviewer. On any disagreement the finding goes to a lawyer, with the first run's account
    kept as a lead."""
    inv = investigate(f, matter, client, max_steps)
    if inv.verdict != "explained":
        return inv

    verdicts = ["explained"]
    for sample in range(1, runs):
        verdicts.append(investigate(f, matter, client, max_steps, sample=sample).verdict)
        if verdicts[-1] != "explained":
            break
    inv.checks["runs"] = verdicts
    if set(verdicts) != {"explained"}:
        inv.verdict, inv.note = "needs_human", f"repeat runs disagreed ({', '.join(verdicts)})"
        return inv

    if verify:
        flagged: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        for other in all_findings:
            if (other.type, other.key) != (f.type, f.key):
                for e in other.evidence:
                    flagged.setdefault(ref(e.doc, e.locator), []).append(other.title)
        # A different model as reviewer is a more independent check than a model marking its own work.
        ok, reason = review_clearance(f, inv, flagged, reviewer or client)
        inv.checks["review"] = {"confirmed": ok, "reason": reason}
        if not ok:
            inv.verdict, inv.note = "needs_human", f"second reviewer did not confirm: {reason}"
    return inv


def investigate_all(findings: list[Finding], matter: Matter, client, runs: int = 3, verify: bool = True,
                    max_steps: int = 10, progress=None, reviewer=None) -> list[Investigation]:
    out = []
    for f in findings:
        inv = investigate_checked(f, matter, client, findings, runs, verify, max_steps, reviewer)
        if progress:
            progress(f, inv)
        out.append(inv)
    return out

==> ./reconciler/models.py <==
"""Core data types. Every Finding carries Evidence that points at a document and a location."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional


@dataclass
class Evidence:
    doc: str        # file name relative to the matter folder
    locator: str    # e.g. "row D-04" or "line 7"
    quote: str      # the exact text relied on


@dataclass
class Finding:
    type: str       # machine-readable category; must have an entry in catalog.CATALOG
    key: str        # stable identifier within the type (used by the evals)
    severity: str   # "high" | "medium" | "low"
    title: str
    detail: str
    evidence: list[Evidence] = field(default_factory=list)
    # Dollars in question, if the finding has a figure. Always positive. Whether it is added
    # to the report's total is decided per type in catalog.CATALOG, not here.
    amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    # Signed effect on (bank balance - stated balance on hand). Lets the report show that the
    # individual items add up to the overall gap. None if the finding does not move the balance.
    balance_effect: Optional[Decimal] = None
    # One or two sentences a client could read. Falls back to the title if empty.
    plain: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        d = asdict(self)
        for k in ("amount", "balance_effect"):
            if d[k] is not None:
                d[k] = f"{d[k]:.2f}"
        return d


@dataclass
class Txn:
    """One money movement, from either the executor's accounts or the bank record."""
    id: str
    date: date
    direction: str  # "in" | "out"
    description: str
    amount: Decimal
    doc: str
    locator: str

    def cite(self) -> Evidence:
        return Evidence(self.doc, self.locator,
                        f"{self.date.isoformat()} | {self.description} | ${self.amount:,.2f}")


@dataclass
class TextDoc:
    """A letter, notice, will or other narrative document, kept line by line for citation."""
    name: str
    lines: list[str]
    doc_type: Optional[str] = None   # a key from extract.DOC_TYPES, if recognised
    date: Optional[date] = None
    reprinted: Optional[date] = None   # date a copy was reprinted, if the document says so


@dataclass
class Matter:
    root: str
    accounts: list[Txn] = field(default_factory=list)
    bank: list[Txn] = field(default_factory=list)
    bank_closing: Optional[Decimal] = None
    bank_doc: Optional[str] = None
    summary: dict[str, tuple[Decimal, Evidence]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    texts: list[TextDoc] = field(default_factory=list)

==> ./reconciler/rules/ledger.py <==
"""Accounts against the bank record, entry by entry."""
from __future__ import annotations

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..models import Finding, Matter, Txn
from ._common import days, fmt, similar


@check
def reconcile_ledgers(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    open_bank = list(m.bank)
    open_acct = list(m.accounts)

    def take(entry: Txn, test) -> Txn | None:
        hits = [b for b in open_bank if b.direction == entry.direction and test(b)]
        if not hits:
            return None
        best = min(hits, key=lambda b: days(entry, b))
        open_bank.remove(best)
        open_acct.remove(entry)
        return best

    # Pass 1: clean matches. Run for every entry first so a clean match is never stolen.
    for e in list(open_acct):
        take(e, lambda b: b.amount == e.amount and days(e, b) <= cfg.exact_window_days)

    # Pass 2: same payee and timing, different amount.
    for e in list(open_acct):
        b = take(e, lambda b: similar(e.description, b.description)
                 and days(e, b) <= cfg.similar_window_days)
        if b:
            sign = 1 if e.direction == "out" else -1
            out.append(Finding(
                "amount_mismatch", e.id, "high",
                f"Accounts entry {e.id} differs from the bank record by {fmt(abs(e.amount - b.amount))}",
                f"The accounts report {fmt(e.amount)}; the bank record shows {fmt(b.amount)} "
                f"for what appears to be the same item.",
                [e.cite(), b.cite()],
                amount=abs(e.amount - b.amount), balance_effect=sign * (e.amount - b.amount),
                plain=f"The accounts give {fmt(e.amount)} for \"{e.description}\". "
                      f"The bank statements show {fmt(b.amount)}."))

    # Pass 3: same amount, but the dates are far apart.
    for e in list(open_acct):
        b = take(e, lambda b: b.amount == e.amount and days(e, b) <= cfg.late_window_days)
        if b:
            out.append(Finding(
                "date_mismatch", e.id, "low",
                f"Accounts entry {e.id} is dated {days(e, b)} days from the bank record",
                f"The accounts date this item {e.date}; the bank record shows {b.date}.",
                [e.cite(), b.cite()],
                amount=e.amount,
                plain=f"\"{e.description}\" ({fmt(e.amount)}) is dated {e.date} in the accounts "
                      f"and {b.date} at the bank, {days(e, b)} days apart."))

    for e in open_acct:
        paid = e.direction == "out"
        out.append(Finding(
            "unsupported_entry", e.id, "high",
            f"No bank transaction supports accounts entry {e.id} ({fmt(e.amount)})",
            "The accounts list this item but no corresponding movement appears in the bank "
            "record produced. A voucher, cheque image or other account statement would resolve it.",
            [e.cite()],
            amount=e.amount, balance_effect=e.amount if paid else -e.amount,
            plain=f"The accounts list a {'payment' if paid else 'receipt'} of {fmt(e.amount)} for "
                  f"\"{e.description}\". The bank statements we were given show no such transaction."))

    for b in open_bank:
        if b.amount < cfg.materiality:
            continue
        left = b.direction == "out"
        out.append(Finding(
            "undisclosed_transaction", b.id, "high",
            f"Bank transaction of {fmt(b.amount)} on {b.date} does not appear in the accounts",
            "The bank record shows this movement but the statement of accounts has no entry for it.",
            [b.cite()],
            amount=b.amount, balance_effect=-b.amount if left else b.amount,
            plain=f"The bank statements show {fmt(b.amount)} {'leaving' if left else 'entering'} the "
                  f"estate account on {b.date} (\"{b.description}\"). The accounts do not mention it."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/bequests.py <==
"""Gifts of money in the will against the payments in the accounts."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from decimal import Decimal

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..extract import statements, tokens
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt

# "I give the sum of $10,000 to the Kingston Public Library Foundation."
GIFT = re.compile(r"\bI (?:give|bequeath|leave)\b[^$]*\$\s?([0-9][0-9,]*(?:\.[0-9]{2})?)\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?(.+?)\s*(?:[.,;]|$)", re.I)


@check
def check_bequests(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    payments = [t for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "out"]
    for doc in m.texts:
        if doc.doc_type != "will":
            continue
        for s in statements(doc):
            hit = GIFT.search(s.text)
            if not hit:
                continue
            amount, name = Decimal(hit.group(1).replace(",", "")), hit.group(2)
            paid = any(t.amount == amount and (tokens(t.description) & tokens(name)
                                               or "bequest" in t.description.lower()
                                               or "legacy" in t.description.lower())
                       for t in payments)
            if not paid:
                out.append(Finding(
                    "unpaid_bequest", f"{amount:.2f}", "medium",
                    f"Will leaves {fmt(amount)} to {name}; the accounts show no payment",
                    "The Will makes this gift but no disbursement of that amount to that recipient "
                    "appears in the accounts. It may not yet be due; the accounts do not say.",
                    [s.cite()],
                    amount=amount,
                    plain=f"The will leaves {fmt(amount)} to {name}. The accounts show no payment "
                          "of this gift so far."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/form_dates.py <==
"""Forms whose own dates disagree: dated on one day, signed months later.

Some forms carry a printed date from when they were prepared and a separate signature date.
A long gap matters when the timing of the filing is in question, because the printed date can
make a late filing look prompt."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..extract import DOC_TYPES
from ..ingest import ISO_DATE, parse_date
from ..models import Evidence, Finding, Matter

SIGNED = re.compile(r"^\s*(signed|date signed|submitted)\b", re.I)


@check
def check_form_dates(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for d in m.texts:
        if not d.date or not d.doc_type:
            continue
        for i, line in enumerate(d.lines[:30], start=1):
            hit = ISO_DATE.search(line)
            if not (SIGNED.search(line) and hit):
                continue
            signed = parse_date(hit.group(1))
            gap = (signed - d.date).days
            if gap > cfg.form_signature_gap_days:
                label = DOC_TYPES[d.doc_type][0]
                out.append(Finding(
                    "document_date_conflict", d.doc_type, "medium",
                    f"The {label} is dated {d.date} but was signed on {signed}",
                    f"The form's own date is {gap} days earlier than its signature date. Which date is "
                    "used affects any account of when the step was taken.",
                    [Evidence(d.name, "header", f"dated {d.date}"), Evidence(d.name, f"line {i}", line.strip())],
                    plain=f"The {label} carries a date of {d.date}, but it was signed on {signed}, "
                          f"{gap} days later. The earlier date could make the step look as if it was "
                          "taken sooner than it was."))
                break
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/invoice_arithmetic.py <==
"""Does a lawyer's invoice add up? Time entries against the fees billed; fees plus tax against the total."""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..invoices import invoices
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt


@check
def check_invoice_arithmetic(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for inv in invoices(m):
        entered = sum((e.amount for e in inv.entries), Decimal(0))
        fees = inv.get("fees")
        if fees is not None and inv.entries and fees != entered:
            diff = abs(fees - entered)
            out.append(Finding(
                "invoice_arithmetic", inv.doc.name, "medium",
                f"Invoice bills fees of {fmt(fees)}; its time entries add up to {fmt(entered)}",
                f"A difference of {fmt(diff)} (before tax) between the fees billed and the "
                f"{len(inv.entries)} time entries listed on the invoice.",
                [inv.stated["fees"][1]],
                amount=diff,
                plain=f"A legal invoice paid by the estate bills {fmt(fees)} in fees, but the work "
                      f"itemised on it adds up to {fmt(entered)}, a difference of {fmt(diff)} before tax."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/compensation.py <==
"""Executor compensation against a configurable starting-point guideline."""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt


@check
def check_compensation(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    comp = [t for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "out" and "compensation" in t.description.lower()]
    if not comp:
        return []
    claimed = sum((t.amount for t in comp), Decimal(0))
    receipts = sum((t.amount for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "in"), Decimal(0))
    disb = sum((t.amount for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "out" and t not in comp), Decimal(0))
    guide = (receipts * cfg.comp_rate_receipts + disb * cfg.comp_rate_disbursements).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
    if claimed <= guide:
        return []
    return [Finding(
        "compensation_review", comp[0].id, "medium",
        f"Executor compensation of {fmt(claimed)} exceeds the configured guideline of {fmt(guide)}",
        f"Guideline used: {cfg.comp_rate_receipts:.1%} of receipts ({fmt(receipts)}) plus "
        f"{cfg.comp_rate_disbursements:.1%} of other disbursements ({fmt(disb)}). This is a "
        "starting point for review, not a cap; courts adjust for the work actually done.",
        [t.cite() for t in comp],
        amount=claimed - guide,
        plain=f"The executor has taken {fmt(claimed)} in compensation. A common starting point for an "
              f"estate of this size is about {fmt(guide)}, which is {fmt(claimed - guide)} less. "
              "Whether the higher figure is justified depends on the work done.")]

==> ./reconciler/rules/correspondence.py <==
"""What the correspondence says against what was actually produced."""
from __future__ import annotations

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..extract import DOC_TYPES, PENDING, PROMISE, statements, tokens
from ..models import Evidence, Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt


@check
def check_correspondence(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    produced = {d.doc_type: d for d in m.texts if d.doc_type}
    if m.accounts:
        produced.setdefault("statement_of_accounts", None)
    if m.bank:
        produced.setdefault("bank_records", None)

    missing: dict[str, list[Evidence]] = {}
    pending: dict[str, list] = {}       # doc type -> [(letter date, evidence)]
    known_amounts = {t.amount for t in m.accounts} | {t.amount for t in m.bank}

    for doc in m.texts:
        if doc.doc_type:          # only correspondence makes representations we test
            continue
        for s in statements(doc):
            for dt in s.doc_types():
                label = DOC_TYPES[dt][0]
                # (a) promised or "enclosed", but not in the production
                if PROMISE.search(s.text) and dt not in produced:
                    missing.setdefault(dt, []).append(s.cite())
                # (b) said to be outstanding after the document's own date
                held = produced.get(dt)
                if (PENDING.search(s.text) and held is not None and held.date and doc.date
                        and doc.date > held.date):
                    pending.setdefault(dt, []).append((doc.date, s.cite()))

            # (c) dollar figures in letters that match nothing in the records
            for amt in s.amounts():
                if amt < cfg.letter_amount_floor or amt in known_amounts:
                    continue
                scored = [(len(tokens(s.text) & tokens(t.description)), t) for t in m.accounts]
                score, best = max(scored, key=lambda x: x[0], default=(0, None))
                if best is not None and score >= 2:
                    out.append(Finding(
                        "letter_vs_records", best.id, "medium",
                        f"Letter states {fmt(amt)}; the accounts record {fmt(best.amount)} for the same item",
                        f"A difference of {fmt(abs(amt - best.amount))} between the correspondence "
                        f"and accounts entry {best.id}.",
                        [s.cite(), best.cite()],
                        amount=abs(amt - best.amount),
                        plain=f"A letter gave {fmt(amt)} as the figure for \"{best.description}\". "
                              f"The accounts record {fmt(best.amount)}, a difference of {fmt(abs(amt - best.amount))}."))

    for dt, letters in pending.items():
        held, label = produced[dt], DOC_TYPES[dt][0]
        letters.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        first, last = letters[0][0], letters[-1][0]
        reprint = (f" The copy produced was reprinted on {held.reprinted}; the original issue date is {held.date}."
                   if held.reprinted else "")
        out.append(Finding(
            "timeline_contradiction", dt, "high",
            f"{len(letters)} letter(s) from {first} to {last} say the {label} is outstanding; it is dated {held.date}",
            f"The document in the production is dated {(first - held.date).days} days before the first letter "
            f"describing it as not yet received.{reprint}",
            [e for _, e in letters] + [Evidence(held.name, "header", f"dated {held.date}"
                                                + (f"; reprinted {held.reprinted}" if held.reprinted else ""))],
            plain=f"{'A letter' if len(letters) == 1 else str(len(letters)) + ' letters'} from the executor's lawyers, "
                  f"the first dated {first}, said the {label} had not yet arrived. The notice itself is dated "
                  f"{held.date}." + (f" The copy we were given is a reprint made on {held.reprinted}, which "
                                     "can make the notice look more recent than it is." if held.reprinted else "")))

    for dt, cites in missing.items():
        label = DOC_TYPES[dt][0]
        out.append(Finding(
            "missing_document", dt, "high",
            f"{label[0].upper() + label[1:]} referred to in correspondence but not produced",
            f"Correspondence says this document is enclosed or will be provided ({len(cites)} "
            "reference(s)); no such document is in the production reviewed.",
            cites,
            plain=f"The executor's lawyers wrote that they were enclosing or would send the {label}. "
                  "It is not among the documents we were given."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/_common.py <==
"""Small helpers shared by the checks."""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal

from ..extract import tokens
from ..models import Matter, Txn


def fmt(x: Decimal) -> str:
    return f"${x:,.2f}"


def days(a: Txn, b: Txn) -> int:
    return abs((a.date - b.date).days)


def similar(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    return len(tokens(a) & tokens(b)) >= 1


def small_bank_items(m: Matter, materiality: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Net money out through bank items too small to flag (monthly fees and the like)."""
    return sum((b.amount if b.direction == "out" else -b.amount
                for b in m.bank if b.amount < materiality), Decimal(0))

==> ./reconciler/rules/tax_payments.py <==
"""Payments to the tax authority on a reassessment, against the notices actually produced."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt

REASSESS = re.compile(r"reassess|\bNORA\b", re.I)
YEAR = re.compile(r"\b(20\d\d)\b")


@check
def check_tax_payments(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    notices = [d for d in m.texts if d.doc_type == "notice_of_reassessment"]
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for t in m.accounts:
        if t.direction != "out" or not REASSESS.search(t.description):
            continue
        year = YEAR.search(t.description)
        amount_text = f"{t.amount:,.2f}"
        supported = any(amount_text in "\n".join(d.lines)
                        and (not year or year.group(1) in "\n".join(d.lines)) for d in notices)
        if not supported:
            out.append(Finding(
                "unsupported_tax_payment", t.id, "medium",
                f"Tax payment of {fmt(t.amount)} on a reassessment, with no matching notice produced",
                "The accounts record a payment on a reassessment, but no Notice of Reassessment for that "
                "amount" + (f" and tax year ({year.group(1)})" if year else "") + " is in the production.",
                [t.cite()],
                amount=t.amount,
                plain=f"The estate paid {fmt(t.amount)} to the tax authority for a reassessment "
                      f"(\"{t.description}\"). We have not been given the notice that says this amount was owed."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/duplicate_time.py <==
"""The same time entry appearing twice on a lawyer's invoice."""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..invoices import invoices
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt


@check
def check_duplicate_time(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for inv in invoices(m):
        groups = defaultdict(list)
        for e in inv.entries:
            if e.amount > 0:
                groups[(e.date, e.timekeeper, e.description.lower(), e.hours)].append(e)
        for same in groups.values():
            if len(same) < 2:
                continue
            e, extra = same[0], sum(x.amount for x in same[1:])
            out.append(Finding(
                "duplicate_time_entry", f"{e.date}:{e.timekeeper}:{e.amount:.2f}", "medium",
                f"Time entry of {e.date} ({fmt(e.amount)}) appears {len(same)} times on the invoice",
                f"The same date, timekeeper, description and hours are billed {len(same)} times. "
                f"The repeat amounts to {fmt(extra)} before tax.",
                [x.evidence for x in same],
                amount=extra,
                plain=f"A legal invoice paid by the estate lists the same work twice on {e.date} "
                      f"(\"{e.description}\"). The repeat is {fmt(extra)} before tax."))
    return out

@check
def check_avoidable_costs(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    return [Finding(
        "avoidable_cost", t.id, "medium",
        f"Estate paid {fmt(t.amount)} described as \"{t.description}\"",
        "A penalty or late charge was paid from estate funds. Whether the estate or the "
        "person responsible for the delay should bear it is a question for counsel.",
        [t.cite()],
        amount=t.amount,
        plain=f"The estate paid {fmt(t.amount)} for \"{t.description}\". Whether the estate "
              "should bear a charge like this is a question for your lawyer.")
        for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "out" and CHARGE.search(t.description)]

==> ./reconciler/rules/agent_fees.py <==
"""A paid agent (such as a trust company) administering the estate, and full executor compensation.

Where an executor hands the work of administration to an agent paid from the estate, the
estate risks paying twice for the same work. Courts may reduce the executor's compensation by
some or all of the agent's fees. How much is a question of judgment, so the agent never decides
this one; it always goes to a lawyer."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from decimal import Decimal

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..extract import statements
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt

AGENT = re.compile(r"\bagent\b|trust company|management fee|administration fee", re.I)
CLAUSE = re.compile(r"\bemploy\b.*\b(agent|trust company)\b", re.I)


@check
def check_agent_fee_offset(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    paid_out = [t for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "out"]
    comp = [t for t in paid_out if "compensation" in t.description.lower()]
    agent = [t for t in paid_out if AGENT.search(t.description) and t not in comp]
    if not comp or not agent:
        return []
    fees = sum((t.amount for t in agent), Decimal(0))
    claimed = sum((t.amount for t in comp), Decimal(0))
    clause = [s.cite() for d in m.texts if d.doc_type == "will" for s in statements(d) if CLAUSE.search(s.text)]
    return [Finding(
        "agent_fee_offset", comp[0].id, "medium",
        f"Agent fees of {fmt(fees)} paid as well as executor compensation of {fmt(claimed)}",
        f"{len(agent)} payment(s) to an agent for administering the estate total {fmt(fees)}. The executor "
        f"also claims {fmt(claimed)} in compensation. Whether compensation should be reduced to reflect "
        "work done by the paid agent is a question for counsel."
        + (" The Will permits employing an agent; that permits the expense, it does not settle the offset."
           if clause else ""),
        [t.cite() for t in agent] + [t.cite() for t in comp] + clause,
        amount=min(fees, claimed),
        plain=f"The estate paid an agent {fmt(fees)} to help administer it, and the executor also took "
              f"{fmt(claimed)} in compensation. Where someone else was paid to do part of the executor's "
              "work, a court may reduce the executor's compensation so the estate does not pay twice. "
              "How much, if anything, is a question for your lawyer.")]

==> ./reconciler/rules/personal_fees.py <==

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..invoices import invoices
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt

PERSONAL = re.compile(r"\bpersonal(ly)?\b|executor'?s? conduct|\bremoval\b|\bobjections?\b", re.I)


@check
def check_personal_fees(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    return [Finding(
        "personal_legal_fees", f"{e.date}:{e.timekeeper}:{e.amount:.2f}", "medium",
        f"Time entry of {e.date} ({fmt(e.amount)}) may be advice to the executor personally",
        "The description suggests work for the executor's own benefit, billed on an invoice the "
        "estate paid. Whether the estate should bear it is a question for counsel. Amount is before tax.",

        amount=e.amount,
        plain=f"A legal invoice paid by the estate includes {fmt(e.amount)} for \"{e.description}\". "
