==> ./reconciler/rules/_template.py <==
"""TEMPLATE. Copy this file to a new name without the leading underscore, then edit.

Checklist (the tests enforce most of it):
  1. One function, decorated with @check, taking (m: Matter, cfg: Config).
  2. Return a list of Finding. Return [] when there is nothing to report.
  3. Every Finding needs at least one Evidence that points at a real file and location.
  4. Do all arithmetic here in code, with Decimal. Never ask a model to add or compare.
  5. Add the new type to reconciler/catalog.py.
  6. Plant one example in make_demo.py (only when seeded=True) and label it in
     evals/ground_truth.json. The clean control file must still raise nothing.
  7. Run:  python verify.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..models import Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt


@check
def my_new_check(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for t in m.accounts:
        if False:  # replace with the condition that makes this entry worth a question
            out.append(Finding(
                type="my_new_type",              # must exist in catalog.CATALOG
                key=t.id,                        # stable, unique within the type
                severity="medium",               # high | medium | low
                title=f"Short statement of the discrepancy ({fmt(t.amount)})",
                detail="What two sources say and where they differ. No conclusions about conduct.",
                evidence=[t.cite()],
                amount=t.amount,                 # dollars in question, or leave out
                plain="One or two sentences a client could read."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/clearance_delay.py <==
"""Time between the final assessment arriving and the clearance certificate being applied for.

An executor usually cannot make a final distribution until the clearance certificate arrives,
and cannot apply for it until the final assessment has been issued. A long gap between the two
holds up the estate and is worth asking about."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..ingest import ISO_DATE, parse_date
from ..models import Evidence, Finding, Matter

SIGNED = re.compile(r"^\s*(signed|date signed|submitted)\b", re.I)


@check
def check_clearance_delay(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    notices = [d for d in m.texts if d.doc_type == "notice_of_assessment" and d.date]
    apps = [d for d in m.texts if d.doc_type == "clearance_application"]
    if not notices or not apps:
        return []
    notice = max(notices, key=lambda d: d.date)
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for app in apps:
        for i, line in enumerate(app.lines[:30], start=1):
            hit = ISO_DATE.search(line)
            if SIGNED.search(line) and hit:
                applied = parse_date(hit.group(1))
                gap = (applied - notice.date).days
                if gap > cfg.clearance_followup_days:
                    out.append(Finding(
                        "clearance_delay", "clearance_application", "medium",
                        f"Clearance certificate applied for {gap} days after the final assessment",
                        f"The Notice of Assessment is dated {notice.date}; the application was signed on "
                        f"{applied}. The configured threshold is {cfg.clearance_followup_days} days.",
                        [Evidence(notice.name, "header", f"dated {notice.date}"),
                         Evidence(app.name, f"line {i}", line.strip())],
                        plain=f"The tax assessment the executor needed was issued on {notice.date}. The "
                              f"application for the clearance certificate was signed on {applied}, {gap} days "
                              "later. Until the certificate arrives, the estate usually cannot be fully paid out."))
                break
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/billing_timeline.py <==
"""A letter says a document has not arrived; the lawyers' own time records show they had it."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..extract import DOC_TYPES, PENDING, statements
from ..invoices import invoices
from ..models import Finding, Matter

HANDLED = re.compile(r"\breview|\breceiv|\breceipt", re.I)


@check
def check_billing_timeline(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    entries = [e for inv in invoices(m) for e in inv.entries]
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for doc in m.texts:
        if doc.doc_type or not doc.date:
            continue
        for s in statements(doc):
            if not PENDING.search(s.text):
                continue
            for dt in s.doc_types():
                label, pattern = DOC_TYPES[dt]
                earlier = [e for e in entries if e.date < doc.date
                           and pattern.search(e.description) and HANDLED.search(e.description)]
                if earlier and dt not in seen:
                    seen.add(dt)
                    e = min(earlier, key=lambda x: x.date)
                    gap = (doc.date - e.date).days
                    out.append(Finding(
                        "billing_contradiction", dt, "high",
                        f"Letter of {doc.date} says the {label} is outstanding; time was billed for it on {e.date}",
                        f"The executor's lawyers recorded time on this document {gap} days before "
                        "their letter describing it as not yet received.",
                        [s.cite(), e.evidence],
                        plain=f"A letter dated {doc.date} said the {label} had not yet arrived. The same "
                              f"law firm's invoice records work on it on {e.date}, {gap} days earlier."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/arithmetic.py <==
"""The accounts' own arithmetic: do the totals add up, and does the balance match the bank?"""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal

from ..checks import Config, check
from ..models import Evidence, Finding, Matter
from ._common import fmt, small_bank_items


@check
def check_arithmetic(m: Matter, cfg: Config) -> list[Finding]:
    out: list[Finding] = []
    sums = {
        "total_receipts": sum((t.amount for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "in"), Decimal(0)),
        "total_disbursements": sum((t.amount for t in m.accounts if t.direction == "out"), Decimal(0)),
    }
    for key, computed in sums.items():
        if key in m.summary:
            stated, ev = m.summary[key]
            if stated != computed:
                name = key.replace("_", " ")
                # Overstated disbursements (or understated receipts) lower the stated balance.
                effect = stated - computed if key == "total_disbursements" else computed - stated
                out.append(Finding(
                    "arithmetic_error", key, "high",
                    f"Stated {name} is off by {fmt(abs(stated - computed))}",
                    f"The summary states {fmt(stated)}; the itemised entries add up to {fmt(computed)}.",
                    [ev],
                    amount=abs(stated - computed), balance_effect=effect,
                    plain=f"The accounts give {fmt(stated)} as the {name}, but the items listed "
                          f"add up to {fmt(computed)}."))

    if "balance_on_hand" in m.summary and m.bank_closing is not None:
        stated, ev = m.summary["balance_on_hand"]
        # Small bank items we chose not to flag (e.g. monthly fees) legitimately explain a gap.
        small = small_bank_items(m, cfg.materiality)
        expected = m.bank_closing + small
        if abs(stated - expected) > Decimal("0.01"):
            out.append(Finding(
                "balance_mismatch", "balance_on_hand", "high",
                f"Stated balance on hand differs from the bank balance by {fmt(abs(stated - expected))}",
                f"The summary states {fmt(stated)}; the bank record closes at {fmt(m.bank_closing)} "
                f"(with {fmt(small)} of sub-materiality bank items allowed for).",
                [ev, Evidence(m.bank_doc or "bank record", "last line", f"closing balance {fmt(m.bank_closing)}")],
                amount=abs(stated - expected),
                plain=f"The accounts say {fmt(stated)} should be on hand. The bank balance is "
                      f"{fmt(m.bank_closing)}, a difference of {fmt(abs(stated - expected))} after small fees."))
    return out

==> ./reconciler/rules/__init__.py <==
"""One file per check. Files starting with "_" are helpers or templates and are not loaded."""

==> ./reconciler/ingest.py <==
"""Load a matter folder.

Conventions (kept deliberately simple; see README):
  accounts*.csv          executor's statement of accounts: entry_id,date,type,description,amount
  bank*.csv              bank record: date,description,debit,credit,balance
  accounts_summary*.txt  the totals the executor reports ("Total receipts: $...")
  anything else .txt/.md/.pdf, in any subfolder: narrative documents (letters, notices, the will)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import re
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

from .extract import classify_doc
from .models import Evidence, Matter, TextDoc, Txn

MONEY = re.compile(r"\$\s?([0-9][0-9,]*(?:\.[0-9]{2})?)")
ISO_DATE = re.compile(r"(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})")
SUMMARY_KEYS = {
    "total receipts": "total_receipts",
    "total disbursements": "total_disbursements",
    "balance on hand": "balance_on_hand",
}


def money(s: str) -> Decimal:
    return Decimal(s.replace("$", "").replace(",", "").strip() or "0")


def parse_date(s: str) -> date:
    return datetime.strptime(s.strip(), "%Y-%m-%d").date()


def _read_text(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    if path.suffix.lower() == ".pdf":
        try:
            from pypdf import PdfReader  # optional dependency
        except ImportError as e:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{path.name}: install pypdf to read PDFs") from e
        text = "\n".join((p.extract_text() or "") for p in PdfReader(str(path)).pages)
        return text.splitlines()
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()


REPRINT = re.compile(r"reprint|reproduc|copy (made|printed)", re.I)


def _doc_date(lines: list[str]) -> Optional[date]:
    """The document's own date: a 'Date:' or 'Date issued:' header line with an ISO date.

    A line describing a reprint or reproduction is skipped. A copy of a notice reprinted in
    July is still a notice issued in December, and reading the reprint date as the issue date
    would hide exactly the contradiction the timeline check exists to find."""
    for line in lines[:15]:
        if line.lower().startswith("date") and not REPRINT.search(line):
            m = ISO_DATE.search(line)
            if m:
                return parse_date(m.group(1))
    return None


def _reprint_date(lines: list[str]) -> Optional[date]:
    for line in lines[:15]:
        if REPRINT.search(line):
            m = ISO_DATE.search(line)
            if m:
                return parse_date(m.group(1))
    return None


def load_matter(root: str | Path) -> Matter:
    root = Path(root)
    m = Matter(root=str(root))
    for path in sorted(p for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()):
        rel = str(path.relative_to(root))
        name, suffix = path.name.lower(), path.suffix.lower()

        if suffix == ".csv" and name.startswith("accounts"):
            with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                    kind = row["type"].strip().lower()
                    m.accounts.append(Txn(
                        id=row["entry_id"].strip(), date=parse_date(row["date"]),
                        direction="in" if kind.startswith("receipt") else "out",
                        description=row["description"].strip(), amount=money(row["amount"]),
                        doc=rel, locator=f"row {row['entry_id'].strip()}"))

        elif suffix == ".csv" and name.startswith("bank"):
            m.bank_doc = rel
            with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for i, row in enumerate(csv.DictReader(f), start=2):  # line 1 is the header
                    debit, credit = money(row["debit"]), money(row["credit"])
                    d = parse_date(row["date"])
                    amount = debit if debit else credit
                    m.bank.append(Txn(
                        id=f"{d.isoformat()}:{amount:.2f}", date=d,
                        direction="out" if debit else "in",
                        description=row["description"].strip(), amount=amount,
                        doc=rel, locator=f"line {i}"))
                    if row.get("balance", "").strip():
                        m.bank_closing = money(row["balance"])

        elif suffix == ".txt" and name.startswith("accounts_summary"):
            for i, line in enumerate(_read_text(path), start=1):
                for label, key in SUMMARY_KEYS.items():
                    if line.lower().startswith(label):
                        found = MONEY.search(line)
                        if found:
                            m.summary[key] = (money(found.group(1)), Evidence(rel, f"line {i}", line.strip()))

        elif suffix in (".txt", ".md", ".pdf"):
            lines = _read_text(path)
            m.texts.append(TextDoc(name=rel, lines=lines,
                                   doc_type=classify_doc(rel, lines), date=_doc_date(lines),
                                   reprinted=_reprint_date(lines)))
    return m

==> ./reconciler/__init__.py <==
"""Estate Reconciler: cross-checks an executor's accounts against bank records and correspondence."""

==> ./reconciler/llm.py <==
"""Optional language-model client for any OpenAI-compatible endpoint.

Works with a local server (Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM), so privileged documents never have to
leave the machine. Configure with environment variables:

    RECONCILER_LLM_BASE_URL   e.g. http://localhost:11434/v1
    RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL      a model that supports tool calling, for the investigator
    RECONCILER_LLM_API_KEY    optional
    RECONCILER_REVIEW_MODEL   optional: a different model to act as second reviewer of clearances
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import urllib.request


def configured() -> bool:
    return bool(os.environ.get("RECONCILER_LLM_BASE_URL") and os.environ.get("RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL"))


class Client:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str | None = None, model: str | None = None,
                 api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 300):
        self.base = (base_url or os.environ["RECONCILER_LLM_BASE_URL"]).rstrip("/")
        self.model = model or os.environ["RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL"]
        self.key = api_key or os.environ.get("RECONCILER_LLM_API_KEY")
        self.timeout = timeout

    def complete(self, messages: list[dict], tools: list[dict] | None = None,
                 temperature: float = 0, seed: int | None = None) -> dict:
        """One chat turn. Returns the assistant message (which may contain tool_calls)."""
        body = {"model": self.model, "temperature": temperature, "messages": messages}
        if seed is not None:
            body["seed"] = seed
        if tools:
            body["tools"] = tools
        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        if self.key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.key}"
        req = urllib.request.Request(f"{self.base}/chat/completions",
                                     data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:
            return json.load(resp)["choices"][0]["message"]


def reviewer_client() -> Client | None:
    """A client for the second reviewer, if a separate model is configured."""
    model = os.environ.get("RECONCILER_REVIEW_MODEL")
    return Client(model=model) if model else None


def chat(system: str, user: str) -> str:
    msg = Client().complete([{"role": "system", "content": system}, {"role": "user", "content": user}])
    return (msg.get("content") or "").strip()

==> ./reconciler/report.py <==
"""Write the outputs: a lawyer's report, a client summary, a schedule (CSV) and JSON.

All figures come from summary.py. Nothing here calls a model. Both reports number the items

import csv

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from html import escape
from pathlib import Path

from .catalog import info

from .summary import OPEN, balance_bridge, statuses, totals

"""
STATUS = {"explained": "Explained by the file", "stands": "Stands after investigation",
          "needs_human": "Needs a lawyer's review", "not_investigated": "Not yet investigated"}
CLIENT_STATUS = {"stands": "To be raised", "not_investigated": "To be raised",
                 "needs_human": "Under review by your lawyer"}
CAVEAT = ("The amount in question is the total of the individual discrepancies. It is not a calculation "
          "of loss or of what anyone owes. Some items may be explained once documents are produced.")


def money(x: Decimal) -> str:
    return f"-${-x:,.2f}" if x < 0 else f"${x:,.2f}"


def write_json(items: list, path: Path) -> None:
    path.write_text(json.dumps([x.to_dict() for x in items], indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def load_investigations(path: Path) -> list:
    from .agent import Investigation
    out = []
    for d in json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
        d["citations"] = [Evidence(**c) for c in d.get("citations", [])]
        out.append(Investigation(**d))
    return out


def _page(title: str, body: list[str]) -> str:
    return (f"<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>{escape(title)}</title>"
            f"<style>{CSS}</style></head><body>" + "\n".join(body) + "</body></html>")


def _category_table(t: dict, plain: bool) -> list[str]:
    rows = ["<table><tr><th>Category</th><th class='n'>Items</th><th class='n'>Amount</th></tr>"]
    for c in t["categories"]:
        amount = money(c["amount"]) if c["has_amount"] else "n/a"
        if c["has_amount"] and not c["counted"]:
            amount += " <span class='small'>(not added)</span>"
        rows.append(f"<tr><td>{escape(c['category'])}</td><td class='n'>{c['count']}</td><td class='n'>{amount}</td></tr>")
    label = "Total amount in question" if plain else "Amount in question (counted items only)"
    rows.append(f"<tr class='tot'><td>{label}</td><td class='n'>{t['open_count']}</td>"
                f"<td class='n'>{money(t['amount_in_question'])}</td></tr></table>")
    return rows


# --- lawyer's report --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def write_lawyer(m: Matter, findings: list[Finding], invs: list | None, path: Path, title: str) -> None:
    status = statuses(findings, invs)
    inv = {(i.type, i.key): i for i in invs or []}
    t = totals(findings, invs)
    by = t["by_status"]
    body = [
        "<div class='draft'>Draft work product. Verify every citation before relying on it.</div>",
        f"<h1>Reconciliation report: {escape(title)}</h1>",
        f"<p class='sub'>Prepared {date.today().isoformat()} &middot; {len(m.accounts)} accounts entries, "
        f"{len(m.bank)} bank transactions, {len(m.texts)} other documents reviewed</p>",
        "<div class='head'>"
        f"<div class='box'><b>{money(t['amount_in_question'])}</b><span>amount in question</span></div>"
        f"<div class='box'><b>{t['open_count']}</b><span>open items</span></div>"
        f"<div class='box'><b>{by['explained']['count']}</b><span>explained by the file"
        + (f" ({money(by['explained']['amount'])} set aside)" if by['explained']['count'] else "") + "</span></div></div>",
        f"<p class='note'>{CAVEAT} Each item is a discrepancy between documents, not a finding of fact or of wrongdoing."
        + ("" if t["investigated"] else " <b>The items have not been investigated yet</b>, so the total may "
           "include items that the file itself explains (a split payment, a reversed entry).") + "</p>",
        "<h2>Summary by category</h2>", *_category_table(t, plain=False),
    ]
    if t["investigated"]:
        body.append("<p class='small'>Of the amount in question, "
                    f"{money(by['stands']['amount'])} stands after investigation ({by['stands']['count']} items) and "
                    f"{money(by['needs_human']['amount'])} needs a lawyer's review ({by['needs_human']['count']} items).</p>")

    bridge = balance_bridge(m, findings)
    if bridge:
        number = {(f.type, f.key): n for n, f in enumerate(findings, start=1)}
        body += ["<h2>Balance bridge</h2>",
                 "<p class='small'>How the individual items add up to the gap between the balance the accounts "
                 "state and the balance at the bank. A residual of zero means the whole gap is traced to listed items.</p>",
                 "<table>",
                 f"<tr><td>Balance on hand stated in the accounts</td><td class='n'>{money(bridge['stated'])}</td></tr>"]
        for f, effect in bridge["lines"]:
            body.append(f"<tr><td>Item {number[(f.type, f.key)]}: {escape(info(f.type).label)}</td><td class='n'>{money(effect)}</td></tr>")
        if bridge["small_items"]:
            body.append(f"<tr><td>Small bank items not flagged (fees)</td><td class='n'>{money(-bridge['small_items'])}</td></tr>")
        body += [f"<tr><td>Unexplained residual</td><td class='n'>{money(bridge['residual'])}</td></tr>",
                 f"<tr class='tot'><td>Closing balance per bank record</td><td class='n'>{money(bridge['bank'])}</td></tr></table>"]

    def item(n: int, f: Finding) -> list[str]:
        amount = f"<span class='amt'>{money(f.amount)}</span>" if f.amount is not None else ""
        out = [f"<div class='f'><h3>{amount}<span class='tag {f.severity}'>{f.severity}</span>{n}. {escape(f.title)}</h3>"
               f"<div class='type'>{escape(info(f.type).label)}"
               + ("" if info(f.type).counted or f.amount is None else " &middot; amount not added to the total")
               + f"</div><p>{escape(f.detail)}</p>"]
        out += [f"<blockquote>{escape(e.quote)}<cite>{escape(e.doc)}, {escape(e.locator)}</cite></blockquote>" for e in f.evidence]
        i = inv.get((f.type, f.key))
        if i:
            out.append(f"<div class='inv {i.verdict}'><b>{STATUS[i.verdict]}</b><br>{escape(i.explanation or i.note)}"
                       + (f"<br><i>Override: {escape(i.note)}</i>" if i.note and i.explanation else ""))
            out += [f"<blockquote>{escape(e.quote)}<cite>{escape(e.doc)}, {escape(e.locator)}</cite></blockquote>" for e in i.citations]
            out.append("</div>")
        return out + ["</div>"]

    numbered = list(enumerate(findings, start=1))
    body.append("<h2>Open items</h2>")
    for n, f in numbered:
        if status[(f.type, f.key)] in OPEN:
            body += item(n, f)
    cleared = [(n, f) for n, f in numbered if status[(f.type, f.key)] == "explained"]
    if cleared:
        body.append("<h2>Explained by the file</h2><p class='small'>Left out of the total. Spot-check the cited lines.</p>")
        for n, f in cleared:
            body += item(n, f)
    body.append("<h2>Method and limits</h2><p class='small'>Matching, sums and date comparisons are done in code. "
                "Where an investigation ran, a language model looked for an innocent explanation in the file; an item is "
                "marked explained only on three agreeing passes, a second review and specific citations. The tool sees "
                "only the documents in the folder. Compensation is compared with a configurable rule of thumb, not with "
                "the law of any jurisdiction.</p>")
    path.write_text(_page(f"Reconciliation report: {title}", body), encoding="utf-8")


# --- client summary ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def write_client(m: Matter, findings: list[Finding], invs: list | None, path: Path, title: str) -> None:
    status = statuses(findings, invs)
    t = totals(findings, invs)
    explained = t["by_status"]["explained"]["count"]
    body = [
        "<div class='draft'>Draft for your lawyer to review before it is sent to you</div>",
        f"<h1>Summary of our review: {escape(title)}</h1>",
        f"<p class='sub'>Prepared {date.today().isoformat()}</p>",
        "<h2>What we did</h2>",
        f"<p>We compared the executor's accounts ({len(m.accounts)} entries) with the bank statements "
        f"({len(m.bank)} transactions) and with {len(m.texts)} other documents, including the letters "
        "from the executor's lawyers. We looked for places where they do not agree.</p>",
        "<h2>What we found</h2>",
        "<div class='head'>"
        f"<div class='box'><b>{t['open_count']}</b><span>points to raise with the executor</span></div>"
        f"<div class='box'><b>{money(t['amount_in_question'])}</b><span>total amount in question</span></div>"
        + (f"<div class='box'><b>{explained}</b><span>points checked and explained</span></div>" if explained else "")
        + "</div>",
        *_category_table(t, plain=True),
        *([] if t["investigated"] else [
            "<p class='note'>This draft was produced before the points were checked for ordinary explanations "
            "in the documents themselves. Some may drop away once that is done.</p>"]),
        "<h2>What this does and does not mean</h2>",
        f"<p>{CAVEAT}</p><p>These are questions, not conclusions. A difference between two documents can have an "
        "ordinary explanation, and nothing here says that anyone acted improperly. The next step is for your "
        "lawyer to ask the executor for the documents and explanations listed in the request letter.</p>",
        "<h2>The points, one by one</h2>",
        "<table><tr><th>No.</th><th>What we found</th><th class='n'>Amount</th><th>Status</th></tr>",
    ]
    for n, f in enumerate(findings, start=1):
        s = status[(f.type, f.key)]
        if s not in OPEN:
            continue
        amount = "n/a" if f.amount is None else money(f.amount) + ("" if info(f.type).counted else "*")
        body.append(f"<tr><td>{n}</td><td>{escape(f.plain or f.title)}</td><td class='n'>{amount}</td>"
                    f"<td>{CLIENT_STATUS[s]}</td></tr>")
    body.append("</table><p class='small'>* Shown for information and not added to the total, because it overlaps "
                "with other points or no money is in doubt.</p>")
    if explained:
        body.append("<h2>Checked and explained</h2><p>These looked like differences at first, but the documents "
                    "themselves account for them. They are not included in the total.</p><ul>")
        body += [f"<li>{escape(f.plain or f.title)}</li>" for f in findings if status[(f.type, f.key)] == "explained"]
        body.append("</ul>")
    path.write_text(_page(f"Summary of our review: {title}", body), encoding="utf-8")


# --- schedule and bundle ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def write_schedule(findings: list[Finding], invs: list | None, path: Path) -> None:
    status = statuses(findings, invs)
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        w = csv.writer(fh)
        w.writerow(["item", "category", "type", "severity", "status", "amount", "added_to_total", "description", "sources"])
        for n, f in enumerate(findings, start=1):
            s = status[(f.type, f.key)]
            added = info(f.type).counted and f.amount is not None and s in OPEN
            w.writerow([n, info(f.type).category, f.type, f.severity, s,
                        "" if f.amount is None else f"{f.amount:.2f}", "yes" if added else "no", f.title,
                        "; ".join(f"{e.doc}, {e.locator}" for e in f.evidence)])


def write_all(m: Matter, findings: list[Finding], invs: list | None, out: Path, title: str) -> list[Path]:
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    write_json(findings, out / "findings.json")
    write_lawyer(m, findings, invs, out / "report_lawyer.html", title)
    write_client(m, findings, invs, out / "report_client.html", title)
    write_schedule(findings, invs, out / "schedule.csv")
    (out / "draft_request_letter.md").write_text(draft_request(findings, invs or []), encoding="utf-8")
    return [out / n for n in ("report_lawyer.html", "report_client.html", "schedule.csv", "draft_request_letter.md")]

==> ./make_demo.py <==
"""Generate the fictional demo matter.

    python make_demo.py                     -> demo_matter/ (planted problems and decoys)
    build(path, seeded=False, decoys=False) -> a fully consistent file; the tests use it as
                                               a true-negative control

"seeded" plants real problems. "decoys" plants things that look like problems to the first
pass but are explained elsewhere in the file (a split payment, a reversed wire, a document
filed under another name). The investigator agent is scored on telling the two apart.

Every person, firm, address and figure here is invented.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path

DATASET_VERSION = "4"   # bump when the planted problems or decoys change; eval runs record it


def build(root: str | Path, seeded: bool = True, decoys: bool = True) -> None:
    root = Path(root)
    (root / "correspondence").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    S, D = seeded, decoys

    comp = "32000.00" if S else "16000.00"
    accounts = [
        ("R-01", "2023-03-20", "receipt", "Transfer in - deceased's chequing account closing balance", "48310.22"),
        ("R-02", "2023-04-11", "receipt", "Life insurance proceeds - Lakeshore Mutual", "25000.00"),
        ("R-03", "2023-09-15", "receipt", "Sale of 14 Alder Lane - net proceeds", "398500.00"),
        ("R-04", "2023-10-02", "receipt", "Sale of vehicle - 2016 Subaru", "9800.00"),
        ("R-05", "2024-02-28", "receipt", "CRA refund - terminal return", "1942.67"),
        ("D-01", "2023-03-28", "disbursement", "Funeral - Hillcrest Funeral Home", "11200.00"),
        ("D-02", "2023-04-05", "disbursement", "Probate fees - Minister of Finance", "2105.00"),
        ("D-03", "2023-05-12", "disbursement", "Property insurance - 14 Alder Lane", "1380.00"),
        ("D-04", "2023-06-20", "disbursement", "Roof repair - Keller Contracting", "8450.00"),
        ("D-05", "2023-07-03", "disbursement", "Property taxes - City of Kingston", "3212.40"),
        ("D-10", "2023-08-01", "disbursement", "Utilities - final hydro account", "412.18"),
        # decoy: one accounts entry, paid by two cheques a fortnight apart
        *([("D-11", "2023-08-14", "disbursement", "House cleanout - Brightway Haulage", "3600.00")] if D else []),
        ("D-06", "2023-09-18", "disbursement", "Legal fees - Whitlock & Pryce LLP", "14690.00"),
        ("D-07", "2023-11-06", "disbursement", "Accounting fees - Brandt CPA", "2825.00"),
        # the will leaves $10,000 to the library; seeded: it is never paid
        *([] if S else [("D-13", "2023-12-01", "disbursement", "Bequest - Kingston Public Library Foundation", "10000.00")]),
        ("D-08", "2024-01-15", "disbursement", "Interim distribution to beneficiaries", "150000.00"),
        # always present: a reassessment payment WITH its notice in the file (must raise nothing)
        ("D-18", "2023-07-20", "disbursement", "CRA - balance owing on reassessment, deceased's 2021 T1 return", "612.40"),
        # seeded: a paid agent administering the estate, as well as full compensation
        *([("D-14", "2023-06-30", "disbursement", "Agent fees - Northshore Trust Company, estate administration", "2400.00"),
           ("D-15", "2023-09-29", "disbursement", "Agent fees - Northshore Trust Company, estate administration", "2400.00"),
           ("D-16", "2023-12-29", "disbursement", "Agent fees - Northshore Trust Company, estate administration", "2400.00"),
           # seeded: a payment on a reassessment whose notice was never produced
           ("D-17", "2023-11-20", "disbursement", "CRA - payment on reassessment, deceased's 2022 T1 return", "1480.00")]
          if S else []),
        ("D-09", "2024-03-01", "disbursement", "Executor compensation", comp),
        # seeded: a penalty for filing the trust return late, paid from the estate
        *([("D-12", "2024-03-15", "disbursement", "CRA - late-filing penalty, T3 return", "350.00")] if S else []),
    ]
    with (root / "accounts.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(["entry_id", "date", "type", "description", "amount"])
        w.writerows(accounts)

    receipts = sum(Decimal(a[4]) for a in accounts if a[2] == "receipt")
    disb = sum(Decimal(a[4]) for a in accounts if a[2] == "disbursement")
    stated_disb = disb + Decimal("5000") if S else disb          # seeded: totals do not add up
    (root / "accounts_summary.txt").write_text(
        "ESTATE OF MARGARET ELLEN HARTWELL - SUMMARY OF ACCOUNTS\n"
        "Period: 2023-03-04 to 2024-03-31\n"
        f"Total receipts: ${receipts:,.2f}\n"
        f"Total disbursements: ${stated_disb:,.2f}\n"
        f"Balance on hand: ${receipts - stated_disb:,.2f}\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # (date, description, debit, credit)
    bank = [
        ("2023-03-20", "TRANSFER IN CLOSED ACCT M HARTWELL", "", "48310.22"),
        ("2023-03-29", "CHQ 101 HILLCREST FUNERAL HOME", "9200.00" if S else "11200.00", ""),
        ("2023-04-05", "CHQ 102 MINISTER OF FINANCE PROBATE", "2105.00", ""),
        ("2023-04-12", "DEPOSIT LAKESHORE MUTUAL LIFE", "", "25000.00"),
        ("2023-04-28", "MONTHLY ACCOUNT FEE", "4.95", ""),
        ("2023-05-12", "PAD NORTHGATE INSURANCE", "1380.00", ""),
        *([] if S else [("2023-06-21", "CHQ 103 KELLER CONTRACTING", "8450.00", "")]),
        ("2023-07-04", "CITY OF KINGSTON TAX PAYMENT", "3212.40", ""),
        ("2023-08-02", "HYDRO BILL PAYMENT", "412.18", ""),
        *([("2023-08-14", "CHQ 106 BRIGHTWAY HAULAGE DEPOSIT", "1800.00", ""),
           ("2023-08-28", "CHQ 107 BRIGHTWAY HAULAGE BALANCE", "1800.00", ""),
           # decoy: a wire sent in error and reversed two days later
           ("2023-09-05", "WIRE OUT REF 77812", "1250.00", ""),
           ("2023-09-07", "REVERSAL WIRE OUT REF 77812", "", "1250.00")] if D else []),
        ("2023-09-15", "WIRE IN WHITLOCK PRYCE TRUST - ALDER LANE", "", "398500.00"),
        ("2023-09-19", "CHQ 104 WHITLOCK PRYCE LLP", "14690.00", ""),
        ("2023-10-02", "DEPOSIT VEHICLE SALE", "", "9800.00"),
        *([("2023-10-20", "E-TRANSFER TO D COLBY", "5000.00", "")] if S else []),
        ("2023-10-31", "MONTHLY ACCOUNT FEE", "4.95", ""),
        ("2023-12-19" if S else "2023-11-07", "CHQ 105 BRANDT CPA", "2825.00", ""),
        ("2024-01-15", "WIRE OUT BENEFICIARY DISTRIBUTION", "150000.00", ""),
        ("2024-02-28", "CANADA REVENUE AGENCY REFUND", "", "1942.67"),
        ("2024-03-01", "TRANSFER TO D COLBY EXECUTOR COMPENSATION", comp, ""),
        ("2023-07-20", "CANADA REVENUE AGENCY PAYMENT", "612.40", ""),
        *([("2023-06-30", "NORTHSHORE TRUST CO AGENCY FEE", "2400.00", ""),
           ("2023-09-29", "NORTHSHORE TRUST CO AGENCY FEE", "2400.00", ""),
           ("2023-12-29", "NORTHSHORE TRUST CO AGENCY FEE", "2400.00", ""),
           ("2023-11-20", "CANADA REVENUE AGENCY PAYMENT", "1480.00", "")] if S else []),
        *([("2024-03-15", "CANADA REVENUE AGENCY T3 PENALTY", "350.00", "")] if S else
          [("2023-12-01", "CHQ 108 KINGSTON PUBLIC LIBRARY FDN", "10000.00", "")]),
    ]
    bank.sort(key=lambda r: r[0])
    balance = Decimal(0)
    with (root / "bank_statement.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(["date", "description", "debit", "credit", "balance"])
        for d, desc, debit, credit in bank:
            balance += Decimal(credit or "0") - Decimal(debit or "0")
            w.writerow([d, desc, debit, credit, f"{balance:.2f}"])

    (root / "will.txt").write_text(
        "LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT OF MARGARET ELLEN HARTWELL\n"
        "Date: 2019-06-03\n\n"
        "I appoint my niece, Diane Colby, as Executor and Trustee of my Estate.\n"
        "I give the sum of $10,000 to the Kingston Public Library Foundation.\n"
        "I authorize my Executor to employ a trust company as agent to assist in administering my Estate, and to pay its fees from my Estate.\n"
        "I direct that the residue of my Estate be divided equally among my grandchildren.\n",
        encoding="utf-8")

    (root / "cra_notice_of_assessment_2023.txt").write_text(
        "NOTICE OF ASSESSMENT - T3 TRUST INCOME TAX\n"
        # seeded: the copy produced is a reprint, and the reprint date comes first on the page
        + ("Date reprinted: 2025-07-02\n" if S else "") +
        "Date issued: 2024-12-17\n"
        "Addressed to: Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell, c/o Northshore Trust Company\n\n"
        "Tax year: 2023\n"
        "Balance owing: $0.00\n", encoding="utf-8")

    if D:
        # decoy: the promised inventory is in the file, but is not called an inventory
        (root / "schedule_a_assets.txt").write_text(
            "SCHEDULE A - ASSETS OF THE ESTATE AT DATE OF DEATH\n"
            "Date: 2023-04-20\n"
            "Prepared by the Executor, Diane Colby\n\n"
            "1. Chequing account, closing balance $48,310.22\n"
            "2. Life insurance policy, Lakeshore Mutual, $25,000.00\n"
            "3. House and lot at 14 Alder Lane, Kingston (to be valued)\n"
            "4. 2016 Subaru\n"
            "5. Household contents\n", encoding="utf-8")

    head = "From: Whitlock & Pryce LLP (counsel to the Executor)\nTo: Adeyemi Law (counsel to a beneficiary)\nRe: Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell\n\n"

    (root / "correspondence" / "2023-05-02_letter.txt").write_text(
        "Date: 2023-05-02\n" + head +
        "Enclosed please find a copy of the Will.\n" +
        ("An inventory of what the deceased owned will be provided under separate cover.\n" if D else "") +
        ("We will provide the appraisal of 14 Alder Lane once it is received from the appraiser.\n" if S else "") +
        "The Executor intends to list the property for sale this summer.\n", encoding="utf-8")

    price = "412,000" if S else "398,500"
    (root / "correspondence" / "2023-10-05_letter.txt").write_text(
        "Date: 2023-10-05\n" + head +
        f"The property at 14 Alder Lane has sold, and the sale generated net proceeds of ${price} to the Estate.\n" +
        ("The Executor will provide the T3 trust returns for 2023 together with her accounting.\n" if S else "") +
        "An interim distribution is expected in the new year.\n", encoding="utf-8")

    late = "2025-06-10" if S else "2024-11-01"   # clean file: letter predates the Notice of Assessment
    (root / "correspondence" / f"{late}_letter.txt").write_text(
        f"Date: {late}\n" + head +
        "A statement of accounts is enclosed.\n"
        "An interim distribution of $150,000 was made in January 2024.\n"
        "The Executor is still waiting on the Notice of Assessment from CRA and cannot apply for the clearance certificate until it arrives.\n",
        encoding="utf-8")
    _invoices(root, S, D)
    _tax_documents(root, S)
    _routine_letters(root)


def _invoice(path: Path, number: str, dated: str, period: str, entries: list[tuple], fees: Decimal | None = None) -> None:
    """Write one invoice. `fees` overrides the billed fees (to plant an invoice that does not add up)."""
    summed = sum(Decimal(e[3]) * Decimal(e[4]) for e in entries)
    fees = summed if fees is None else fees
    tax = (fees * Decimal("0.13")).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
    lines = [f"INVOICE {number} - WHITLOCK & PRYCE LLP", f"Date: {dated}",
             "To: Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell, c/o Diane Colby, Executor", f"For services: {period}", "",
             "Date | Timekeeper | Description | Hours | Rate | Amount"]
    for d, who, desc, hours, rate in entries:
        lines.append(f"{d} | {who} | {desc} | {Decimal(hours):.2f} | {Decimal(rate):.2f} | {Decimal(hours) * Decimal(rate):.2f}")
    lines += ["", f"Fees: ${fees:,.2f}", f"HST (13%): ${tax:,.2f}", f"Total due: ${fees + tax:,.2f}"]
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _invoices(root: Path, S: bool, D: bool) -> None:
    (root / "invoices").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    JW, LC = "450", "200"
    first = [
        ("2023-03-10", "JW", "Initial meeting with executor; review will", "2.0", JW),
        ("2023-03-22", "LC", "Prepare probate application", "4.0", LC),
        ("2023-04-04", "JW", "Review and file probate application", "1.5", JW),
        # seeded: fewer hours here, so the entries no longer add up to the fees billed
        ("2023-04-18", "JW", "Estate administration advice; correspondence with insurers and bank", "1.5" if S else "5.0", JW),
        ("2023-05-02", "JW", "Letter to beneficiary counsel enclosing will", "0.5", JW),
        ("2023-06-14", "JW", "Telephone call with realtor re listing of 14 Alder Lane", "0.5", JW),
        # seeded: the same entry billed twice, with no credit
        *([("2023-06-14", "JW", "Telephone call with realtor re listing of 14 Alder Lane", "0.5", JW)] if S else []),
        ("2023-07-11", "JW", "Review offer; draft amendments to agreement of purchase and sale", "1.0", JW),
        # decoy: billed twice, but credited at the foot of the invoice
        *([("2023-07-11", "JW", "Review offer; draft amendments to agreement of purchase and sale", "1.0", JW)] if D else []),
        # seeded: advice to the executor in her own interest, on the estate's invoice
        *([("2023-07-25", "JW", "Advise executor personally re beneficiary's complaint about delay", "2.0", JW)] if S else []),
        ("2023-08-01", "JW", "Negotiations with purchaser's solicitor", "4.5", JW),
        ("2023-08-22", "JW", "Review title and requisitions", "3.0", JW),
        ("2023-09-05", "LC", "Statement of adjustments; trust ledger", "5.5", LC),
        ("2023-09-12", "LC", "Prepare closing documents", "6.0", LC),
        ("2023-09-15", "JW", "Attend closing of sale of 14 Alder Lane", "4.0", JW),
        *([("2023-09-16", "JW", "Courtesy credit: entry of 2023-07-11 recorded twice", "-1.0", JW)] if D else []),
    ]
    # The estate paid $14,690.00 (accounts entry D-06): fees of $13,000.00 plus HST.
    _invoice(root / "invoices" / "invoice_whitlock_pryce_2023-09.txt", "2023-0917", "2023-09-16",
             "2023-03-10 to 2023-09-16", first, fees=Decimal("13000.00"))

    second = [
        # seeded: the firm bills for reviewing the notice five months before writing that it had not arrived
        ("2025-01-08", "JW", "Receipt and review of CRA Notice of Assessment; email to executor" if S
         else "Telephone call to CRA re status of assessment", "0.4", "475"),
        ("2025-02-12", "JW", "Correspondence with beneficiary counsel re status of administration", "0.6", "475"),
        ("2025-03-04", "LC", "Update estate ledger; file maintenance", "2.0", "210"),
    ]
    _invoice(root / "invoices" / "invoice_whitlock_pryce_2025-03.txt", "2025-0331", "2025-03-31",
             "2024-12-01 to 2025-03-31 (not yet paid; after the accounting period)", second)


def _tax_documents(root: Path, S: bool) -> None:
    (root / "cra_notice_of_reassessment_2021.txt").write_text(
        "NOTICE OF REASSESSMENT - T1 INDIVIDUAL INCOME TAX\n"
        "Date issued: 2023-06-22\n"
        "Taxpayer: Margaret Ellen Hartwell (deceased)\n\n"
        "Tax year: 2021\n"
        "Amount owing: $612.40\n", encoding="utf-8")
    # seeded: printed date in January, signed in July. Clean: signed within the week.
    (root / "application_for_clearance_certificate.txt").write_text(
        "APPLICATION FOR CLEARANCE CERTIFICATE\n"
        "Date on form: 2025-01-06\n"
        "Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell\n"
        "Applicant: Diane Colby, Executor\n\n"
        "Distribution of property is planned after the certificate is issued.\n\n"
        f"Signed by the Executor: {'2025-07-08' if S else '2025-01-10'}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    if S:
        # seeded: a second letter saying the notice is outstanding, three months after it was issued
        (root / "correspondence" / "2025-03-14_letter.txt").write_text(
            "Date: 2025-03-14\n"
            "From: Whitlock & Pryce LLP (counsel to the Executor)\n"
            "To: Adeyemi Law (counsel to a beneficiary)\n"
            "Re: Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell\n\n"
            "The Executor is awaiting the Notice of Assessment from CRA.\n"
            "We will write again once it has been received.\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _routine_letters(root: Path) -> None:
    """Ordinary correspondence with nothing wrong in it. Present in every version of the file,
    so the clean control also proves that routine letters raise nothing."""
    ours = "From: Adeyemi Law (counsel to a beneficiary)\nTo: Whitlock & Pryce LLP (counsel to the Executor)\nRe: Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell\n\n"
    theirs = "From: Whitlock & Pryce LLP (counsel to the Executor)\nTo: Adeyemi Law (counsel to a beneficiary)\nRe: Estate of Margaret Ellen Hartwell\n\n"
    letters = [
        ("2023-03-15", theirs, "We act for Diane Colby, the Executor named in the late Mrs. Hartwell's testamentary documents.\n"
                               "An application for probate is being prepared. We will write again once probate has been granted.\n"),
        ("2023-03-24", ours, "We act for Thomas Hartwell, a grandchild of the deceased and a residuary beneficiary.\n"
                             "Please keep us informed of the progress of the administration.\n"),
        ("2023-04-21", theirs, "Probate was granted on 5 April 2023.\n"
                               "The Executor has opened an estate account and is gathering in the assets.\n"),
        ("2023-06-28", theirs, "The property at 14 Alder Lane was listed for sale on 26 June 2023.\n"
                               "Repairs to the roof were completed before the listing.\n"),
        ("2023-08-30", ours, "Thank you for your letter of 28 June.\n"
                             "Please confirm the expected closing date for the sale of the property.\n"),
        ("2023-09-20", theirs, "The sale of 14 Alder Lane closed on 15 September 2023 as scheduled.\n"
                               "We will report on the proceeds shortly.\n"),
        ("2024-01-18", theirs, "The interim distribution was paid on 15 January 2024.\n"
                               "Please have your client sign and return the form of receipt.\n"),
        ("2024-06-12", ours, "We have had no update since January.\n"
                             "Please advise when the estate's tax filings are expected to be complete.\n"),
    ]
    for dated, header, body in letters:
        (root / "correspondence" / f"{dated}_letter.txt").write_text(f"Date: {dated}\n" + header + body, encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import shutil
    target = Path(__file__).parent / "demo_matter"
    shutil.rmtree(target, ignore_errors=True)
    build(target)
    print("wrote demo_matter/")

==> ./README.md <==
# Estate Reconciler

Cross-checks an executor's statement of accounts against the bank record and the
correspondence, investigates each discrepancy, and produces a lawyer's report, a client
summary and a draft document-request letter. Every item cites the document and line it
rests on, and the reports put a dollar figure on what is in question.

It works in two stages.

**Stage 1, the checks (plain code).** Raises every discrepancy it can see:

- accounts entries with no bank transaction behind them, and the reverse
- amount and date differences between the two
- totals that do not add up; a stated balance that does not match the bank
- documents a letter says are enclosed or coming that are not in the file
- a letter describing a document as outstanding after the date on the document
- figures quoted in letters that do not match the records
- compensation above a configurable starting point
- gifts of money in the will with no payment in the accounts
- penalties and late charges paid out of the estate
- a lawyer's invoice whose time entries do not add up to the fees billed
- the same time entry billed twice
- time that looks like advice to the executor personally, billed to the estate
- a letter saying a document has not arrived when the firm's own time records show work on it
- a form dated months before it was signed, and a long gap between the final tax assessment
  and the application for the clearance certificate
- a paid agent (such as a trust company) administering the estate alongside full executor
  compensation
- a tax payment on a reassessment with no matching notice in the file

**Stage 2, the investigator (an agent).** For each finding, a model with read-only tools
looks for an innocent explanation already in the file: a split payment, a reversed entry,
a document filed under another name. It returns `explained`, `stands` or `needs_human`,
with citations. Findings that are not explained go into a draft request letter for a
lawyer to review. Nothing is sent.

## Run it

Python 3.10+, no dependencies.

```
python make_demo.py                                   # writes the fictional demo matter
python -m reconciler analyze demo_matter --out out    # stage 1: checks, then the reports
python verify.py                                      # tests + stage 1 eval + reports, one command
python -m reconciler ask demo_matter "Were the T3 returns ever provided?"
python evals/run_eval.py                              # stage 1 scores
python -m unittest discover tests
```

Stage 2 and model-written answers for `ask` need an OpenAI-compatible endpoint. A local
one keeps privileged documents on your own machine. The model must support tool calling.

```
export RECONCILER_LLM_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434/v1     # e.g. Ollama
export RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL=<a tool-calling model you have pulled>

python -m reconciler investigate demo_matter --out out   # reports with verdicts + draft letter
python -m reconciler report demo_matter --out out        # rebuild the reports without re-running the model
python evals/run_agent_eval.py                           # stage 2 scores, saved to evals/runs/
python evals/run_agent_eval.py --runs 1 --no-verify      # the bare agent, for comparison
export RECONCILER_REVIEW_MODEL=<a different model>       # optional: independent second reviewer
python evals/make_table.py --write                       # rebuild the results table below
```

A lawyer-facing explanation of the output is in [GUIDE.md](GUIDE.md).

## What it produces

| File | For | Contents |
|---|---|---|
| `report_lawyer.html` | the lawyer | dollar total, table by category, balance bridge, every item with its citations and verdict |
| `report_client.html` | the client, after the lawyer has reviewed it | the same items and numbering in plain language, no citations |
| `schedule.csv` | working papers | one row per item: category, status, amount, whether it is in the total, sources |
| `draft_request_letter.md` | the lawyer | a request for each open item, from fixed wording |
| `findings.json`, `investigations.json` | audit | everything above in machine-readable form, including each tool call the agent made |

**The dollar figure.** The "amount in question" adds up the open discrepancies. Two rules
keep it defensible. Nothing is counted twice: a wrong total and a balance gap are symptoms
of the items behind them, so they are shown but not added. And anything the investigator
found to be explained by the file is left out. It is not a damages figure, and both
reports say so.

**The balance bridge.** The lawyer's report shows, in code-computed arithmetic, how the
individual items add up to the gap between the stated balance and the bank balance. On
the demo matter the unexplained residual is $0.00. A residual that is not zero means
something moved the balance that no check has identified.

## Extending it

Each check is one file in `reconciler/rules/`. Adding one does not mean editing any
existing check. `AGENTS.md` has the recipe, the rules that must not be broken, and a
backlog of small, self-contained tasks. It is written so that a coding assistant, including
a small local model, can take one task at a time; `python verify.py` tells it whether the
change is safe.

## Design choices

**Arithmetic is never delegated to a model.** Matching, sums and date comparisons are
plain code (`checks.py`). The agent has an `add_amounts` tool for the same reason.

**The model decides where to look; code decides what it may conclude** (`agent.py`):

- `explained` must cite at least one passage that was not already in the finding.
- Every citation must be a reference a tool actually returned in that investigation.
  An invented source turns the verdict into `needs_human`.
- No verdict within the step limit, a model error, or an unrecognised verdict: `needs_human`.
- Questions of judgment (is the compensation reasonable?) go to a lawyer without asking
  the model at all.
- An arithmetic error or a balance gap can never be marked `explained`. The model's
  account is kept as a lead for the reviewer.
- Clearing takes more than keeping. If a first run keeps a finding, that is accepted. If
  it clears one, two further runs (sampled, fixed seeds) must agree, and then a second
  reviewer must confirm. The reviewer is a fresh call that sees only the finding, the
  claim and the cited evidence, and is told by code which citations are themselves
  relied on by another finding. Any dissent sends the item to a lawyer. Set
  `RECONCILER_REVIEW_MODEL` to have a different model act as reviewer, so that no model
  marks its own work.

**The errors are not symmetric.** Sending an explained item to a lawyer costs minutes.
Clearing a real one loses it. Every fallback lands on `needs_human`.

**Every step is auditable.** Each investigation records every tool call and result
(`investigations.json`), so a reviewer can see what the agent looked at, not only what
it concluded.

**The reports are built by code.** Totals, categories and the bridge come from `summary.py`;
no model writes or sums anything in them.

**The letter is a template, not generated prose.** A draft built from fixed wording
cannot introduce a fact that is not in the findings.

**Findings are questions, not accusations.** The report describes what two documents say
and where they differ. It does not characterise anyone's conduct.

## How it is tested

The demo matter contains real problems and decoys (counts in the table below), all labelled in
`evals/ground_truth.json`. A decoy looks like a problem to stage 1 and is explained
elsewhere in the file.

Stage 1, the checks (plain code, no model). Generated by `python evals/make_table.py --write`:

<!-- stage1:start -->
| Measure | Result |
|---|---|
| Labelled items in the demo matter (dataset 4) | 21 real problems, 6 decoys |
| Raises every labelled item, nothing else | 27 of 27, 0 unexpected |
| Share of raised items that are real problems | 78% (21 of 27), by design |
| Control file with no problems (17 documents besides the ledgers) | 0 findings |
<!-- stage1:end -->

Stage 2, early runs on the first version of the demo (11 real problems, 5 decoys; local models, 2026-11-16; recorded by hand, before runs were saved to file):

| Model | Agent version | Real problems wrongly dismissed | Decoys cleared | Sent to a lawyer | Real share of what remains |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `qwen3.8:27b` (dense) | v1: 8 steps, no stated budget | 0 of 11 | 5 of 5 | 5 (1 by rule, 1 by the model, 3 at the step limit) | 100% (11 of 11) |
| `qwen3.8:27b` (dense) | v2: 10 steps, budget stated, last-step warning | 0 of 11 | 5 of 5 | 2 (1 by rule, 1 by the model) | 100% (11 of 11) |
| `qwen3.6:35b` (MoE) | v2 | **2 of 11** | 2 of 5 | 1 (by rule) | 75% (9 of 12) |

One run per row. The v1 and v2 rows for the dense model differ in the agent, not in luck:
v1 ran out of steps on three findings where the right answer was "nothing explains this",
and v2 fixed that.

The MoE row is the most useful result here. That model marked the $5,000 total error and
the balance gap as "explained", the planted trap described below, despite a prompt rule
written to prevent exactly that. Its citations were real, so the citation guardrails let
the verdict through. The response was a rule in code, not a firmer prompt: those two
kinds of finding can no longer be cleared by any model (agent v3). Agent v4 then made
every clearance need three agreeing runs and a second reviewer.

Stage 2, current agent. This table is also generated from the files in `evals/runs/` by
`python evals/make_table.py --write`; nothing in it is typed by hand.

<!-- results:start -->
_No runs saved yet. Run `python evals/run_agent_eval.py`._
<!-- results:end -->

The stage 2 tests in `tests/test_agent.py` use a scripted stand-in for the model. They
prove the loop, tools and guardrails behave, and that each decoy's explanation is
reachable with the tools provided. They say nothing about how well a real model
investigates. That is what `run_agent_eval.py` measures. One run on one small file is a
first reading, not a benchmark.

The file also contains eight routine letters with nothing wrong in them, so that not
every document is a problem. Three planted traps test whether a model mistakes something
related for an explanation: a letter saying the roof repairs were completed (not evidence
that the estate paid for them), a $150,000 distribution to beneficiaries (not the $10,000
gift to the library), and the one below.

One planted trap to watch: the summary overstates disbursements by exactly $5,000, the
same size as an e-transfer missing from the accounts. A model may call one an
explanation of the other. It is a link between two problems, not an explanation of either.

**Reprints are not issue dates.** A reprinted notice can carry its reprint date above its
issue date. Read naively, a notice issued in December and reprinted in July looks as if it
arrived in July, and the letters that called it outstanding in between look accurate.
`ingest.py` skips reprint lines when dating a document, and the demo puts the reprint date
first on the page to test exactly that.

## What the evals have caught so far

- Stage 1's first run scored 82%. A CRA Notice of Assessment whose title mentions "T3"
  was classified as a T3 return, hiding two findings at once. Fixed in `extract.classify_doc`.
- A test stand-in that tried to dismiss a real problem by citing the finding's own
  evidence was overridden by the new-evidence rule. The test had to be rewritten to get
  a wrong dismissal past it.

- Stage 2's first run hit the step limit on three real findings. Each was a case where the
  right answer is "nothing explains this", and the model kept searching instead of
  concluding. The agent is now told its budget up front and warned before its last step.
- A second local model cleared two real problems by citing a third of the same size.
  A prompt rule did not stop it; a code rule now does. The wider weakness remains: for
  other kinds of finding, a model can still present a related problem as an explanation.
  The second reviewer (v4) is aimed at that; whether it works is for the next runs to show.

## Limits

- Stage 1's scores are on a file whose problems and checks were written together. They
  show the pipeline works end to end, not how it performs on real productions.
- Stage 2 has single runs on two models, and the results differ sharply by model. Which
  model is used is a safety decision, not a detail. The decoys were written to be findable with the tools
  provided; real explanations will be harder to find.
- Input is clean CSV and text. Real bank statements arrive as scanned PDFs; OCR and table
  extraction are the hard part and are not here.
- Letter analysis in stage 1 is pattern-based. It misses paraphrase and is confused by
  abbreviations that end in a full stop.
- One bank account, one currency.
- The compensation guideline is a configurable rule of thumb, not a statement of the law
  in any jurisdiction.

## Roadmap

1. Several runs per model on agent v4, with and without the extra checks, to measure what
   they add and what they cost in decoys no longer cleared.
2. A second matter written by someone else, as a held-out test.
3. An LLM extractor behind `extract.statements()`, scored against the rule-based baseline.
4. PDF and scanned-statement ingestion.

## Disclosure

All names, firms, addresses and figures in the demo matter are invented. The code was
written with substantial assistance from Claude (Anthropic); the problem definition,
the checks chosen and the review of the output are mine.

All rights reserved. You may read and run this code to evaluate it; any other use needs
written permission. See [LICENSE](LICENSE).

==> ./AGENTS.md <==
# Working on this project (for people and coding assistants)

Read this file before changing anything. It is written so that one small task can be done
at a time, by a person or by a coding assistant of any size.

## The one command

```
python verify.py
```

It regenerates the demo matter, runs the tests, runs the stage 1 eval and builds the
reports. It needs no model. **A change is not finished until this prints PASS.** If it
fails, fix the cause; do not edit a test or `evals/ground_truth.json` to make it pass
unless the task says the expected result has changed.

## Map

| Path | What it holds |
|---|---|
| `reconciler/rules/*.py` | One check per file. This is where most new work goes. |
| `reconciler/rules/_template.py` | Copy this to start a new check. |
| `reconciler/catalog.py` | One entry per finding type: label, report category, whether its amount is added to the total, request-letter wording. |
| `reconciler/checks.py` | Registry and `Config` (tolerances). Loads every rule file automatically. |
| `reconciler/summary.py` | Dollar totals and the balance bridge. |
| `reconciler/report.py` | Lawyer report, client summary, CSV schedule. |
| `reconciler/agent.py`, `tools.py` | The investigator and its read-only tools. |
| `reconciler/ingest.py`, `extract.py` | Loading a matter folder; turning letters into statements. |
| `reconciler/invoices.py` | Parses a lawyer's invoice (text) into time entries and stated totals. |
| `make_demo.py` | Builds the fictional demo matter. `seeded` plants real problems, `decoys` plants look-alikes. |
| `evals/ground_truth.json` | The label for every finding the demo matter should raise. |
| `tests/` | Unit tests. `test_agent.py` uses a scripted stand-in for the model. |

## Rules that must not be broken

1. **Code does the arithmetic.** Sums, matching and date comparisons are written in Python
   with `Decimal`. A model is never asked to add, compare or reconcile.
2. **Every finding cites evidence.** At least one `Evidence` with a real file and location.
3. **Findings describe documents, not people.** Say what two sources show and where they
   differ. No words like fraud, theft, hid or lied.
4. **Unsafe outcomes fall to a human.** Any new error path in the agent must end in
   `needs_human`, never in `explained`.
5. **No double counting.** If a new finding's amount overlaps with other findings, set
   `counted=False` for its type in the catalog.
6. **The clean control stays clean.** `build(seeded=False, decoys=False)` must raise nothing.
7. **Only fictional data.** Never add a real person, firm, account or document to this repo.
8. **No new dependencies** without a reason written in the README. The core runs on the
   standard library.

## Recipe: add a check

1. Copy `reconciler/rules/_template.py` to `reconciler/rules/<name>.py` and write the check.
   Set `amount` if there is a dollar figure, and `plain` (a sentence a client could read).
   If the finding changes the cash balance, set `balance_effect`; see `ledger.py` for the sign.
2. Add the finding type to `reconciler/catalog.py`.
3. In `make_demo.py`, plant one example that appears only when `seeded=True`. Keep the
   books consistent: if you add an accounts entry, add the matching bank line unless the
   mismatch is the point.
4. Add the expected finding to `evals/ground_truth.json` with `"label": "real"`.
5. If the demo changed, bump `DATASET_VERSION` in `make_demo.py`.
6. Run `python evals/make_table.py --write` to regenerate the README counts, and add the
   check to the lists in `README.md` and `GUIDE.md`.
7. Run `python verify.py`.

To add a decoy instead (something that looks wrong but is explained in the file): plant
it when `decoys=True`, label it `"decoy"`, and add a line to
`test_tools_can_reach_every_decoy_explanation` proving the explanation can be found with
the agent's tools. An unfindable decoy makes the eval unfair.

## When the agent changes

Any change to the prompt or to a rule in `agent.py`: bump `AGENT_VERSION`, add a test for
the behaviour in `tests/test_agent.py`, then re-run `python evals/run_agent_eval.py` and
`python evals/make_table.py --write`. Never type a result into the README by hand.

## Backlog: small tasks, one at a time

Each is sized to be done alone. "Done when" is the acceptance test.

1. **Duplicate payment.** Two bank debits to the same payee for the same amount within 30
   days, with one accounts entry. New rule file. *Done when:* a seeded duplicate is raised
   as `duplicate_payment`, counted, and `verify.py` passes.
2. **Payment to the executor.** Flag any bank debit whose description names the executor
   (take the name from the will's "I appoint ... as Executor" line) and that is not
   described as compensation. *Done when:* the demo's $5,000 e-transfer gains a second,
   uncounted finding `payment_to_executor`, and the catalog marks it `counted=False`.
3. **Distribution check.** If the will divides the residue "equally among" N named people,
   compare with distribution entries. Needs names in the will first. *Done when:* an
   unequal seeded distribution is raised.
4. **Text PDFs.** `ingest.py` already reads `.pdf` letters when `pypdf` is installed. Add a
   test with a generated PDF letter. *Done when:* a PDF letter produces the same findings
   as its `.txt` twin.
5. **Looser promise wording.** Extend `PROMISE` in `extract.py` to catch "should reach you
   shortly" and "under separate cover". Add one seeded example and one sentence that must
   NOT match. *Done when:* both are tested.
6. **Second bank account.** Let `bank*.csv` be several files, each with its own closing
   balance. *Done when:* a transfer between two estate accounts raises nothing.
7. **Configurable guideline.** Read `Config` overrides from an optional `config.json` in
   the matter folder. *Done when:* a 2% rate in the file changes the compensation finding.
8. **Invoice against the accounts.** Compare each invoice's "Total due" with the accounts
   entry that pays it (same firm name, nearest date). *Done when:* a seeded difference is
   raised as `invoice_vs_accounts` and the clean control stays clean.
9. **Hours times rate.** Flag a time entry whose amount is not hours x rate. *Done when:*
   one seeded entry is raised and counted.
10. **Compensation threats in letters.** Flag a letter that says compensation will rise if a
    passing of accounts is required, quoting it neutrally. *Done when:* one seeded sentence is
    raised, one sentence merely mentioning compensation is not, and the finding goes to a
    lawyer by rule (add its type to `JUDGMENT_ONLY`).
11. **Second demo matter.** A new estate with different names and at least three new traps,
   written by someone who has not read `rules/`. This is the most valuable item on the list.

Not on the list on purpose: a web interface, a vector database, more agents. None of them
changes what the tool can prove.

## Using a small model as the coding assistant

- Give it one backlog item and this file. Not the whole repository.
- Point it at the two or three files the task names, plus `_template.py`.
- Have it run `python verify.py` and paste the output. Accept nothing without PASS.
- Read the diff yourself. Check rule 3 (no accusations) and rule 5 (no double counting);
  those are judgment calls a test cannot fully catch.

==> ./evals/make_table.py <==
"""Build the README's results tables, so no number in them is typed by hand.

    python evals/make_table.py            # print both tables
    python evals/make_table.py --write    # replace both blocks in README.md between their markers
    python evals/make_table.py --check    # exit 1 if README.md is out of date (verify.py runs this)

Stage 1 is computed fresh: the demo matter is rebuilt in a temporary folder and scored against
evals/ground_truth.json, and the clean control is built and checked. Stage 2 comes from the
saved runs in evals/runs/.

Runs are grouped by model and agent configuration. Where a group has several runs, the worst
case is shown for wrongly-dismissed (the number that must be zero) and the range for the rest.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
START, END = "<!-- results:start -->", "<!-- results:end -->"
S1_START, S1_END = "<!-- stage1:start -->", "<!-- stage1:end -->"


def build_stage1_table() -> str:
    from evals.run_eval import load_truth, score
    from make_demo import DATASET_VERSION, build
    from reconciler.checks import run_all
    from reconciler.ingest import load_matter

    truth = load_truth()
    real = sum(1 for v in truth.values() if v == "real")
    decoys = len(truth) - real
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        build(tmp)
        r = score(Path(tmp))
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        build(tmp, seeded=False, decoys=False)
        m = load_matter(tmp)
        clean = len(run_all(m))
        docs = len(m.texts)
    raised_real = round(r["precision"] * r["raised"])
    return "\n".join([
        "| Measure | Result |",
        "|---|---|",
        f"| Labelled items in the demo matter (dataset {DATASET_VERSION}) | {real} real problems, {decoys} decoys |",
        f"| Raises every labelled item, nothing else | {r['expected'] - len(r['missed'])} of {r['expected']}, "
        f"{len(r['unexpected'])} unexpected |",
        f"| Share of raised items that are real problems | {r['precision']:.0%} ({raised_real} of {r['raised']}), by design |",
        f"| Control file with no problems ({docs} documents besides the ledgers) | {clean} findings |",
    ])


def _replace(text: str, start: str, end: str, block: str) -> str:
    if start not in text or end not in text:
        sys.exit(f"README.md is missing the markers {start} ... {end}")
    head, rest = text.split(start, 1)
    return head + start + "\n" + block + "\n" + end + rest.split(end, 1)[1]


def _span(values: list[int]) -> str:
    return str(values[0]) if min(values) == max(values) else f"{min(values)} to {max(values)}"


def _reviewer(c: dict) -> str:
    if not c["verify"]:
        return "no second reviewer"
    who = c.get("reviewer", "same model")
    return "second reviewer" if who == "same model" else f"second reviewer `{who}`"


def build_table(runs_dir: Path) -> str:
    groups: dict[tuple, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    for path in sorted(runs_dir.glob("*.json")):
        run = json.loads(path.read_text())
        if not isinstance(run, dict) or "score" not in run:
            continue                      # not a run file in the current format
        c = run.get("config") or {}
        label = (f"{c['agent']}, demo set {c.get('dataset', '1')}, {c['runs']} run(s) to clear, "
                 f"{_reviewer(c)}") if c else "not recorded"
        groups[(run.get("model", "?"), label)].append(run["score"])
    if not groups:
        return "_No runs saved yet. Run `python evals/run_agent_eval.py`._"

    rows = ["| Model | Agent configuration | Eval runs | Real problems wrongly dismissed (worst run) "
            "| Decoys cleared | Sent to a lawyer |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for (model, label), scores in sorted(groups.items()):
        real, decoys = scores[0]["real"], scores[0]["decoys"]
        worst = max(len(s["wrongly_dismissed"]) for s in scores)
        cleared = _span([len(s["decoys_cleared"]) for s in scores])
        human = _span([s["sent_to_human"] for s in scores])
        rows.append(f"| `{model}` | {label} | {len(scores)} | {worst} of {real} | {cleared} of {decoys} | {human} |")
    return "\n".join(rows)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    stage1, stage2 = build_stage1_table(), build_table(ROOT / "evals" / "runs")
    print(stage1, stage2, sep="\n\n")
    readme = ROOT / "README.md"
    text = readme.read_text()
    updated = _replace(_replace(text, S1_START, S1_END, stage1), START, END, stage2)
    if "--check" in sys.argv and updated != text:
        sys.exit("\nREADME.md results are out of date: run  python evals/make_table.py --write")
    if "--write" in sys.argv:
        readme.write_text(updated)
        print("\nREADME.md updated" if updated != text else "\nREADME.md already current")
