==> ./evals/run_agent_eval.py <==
"""Stage 2: score the investigator agent. Needs a model (see README).

    python evals/run_agent_eval.py                      # full agent: 3 agreeing runs + second reviewer
    python evals/run_agent_eval.py --runs 1 --no-verify # the bare agent, to measure what the checks add

Each run is saved to evals/runs/<model>_<time>.json. `python evals/make_table.py` turns
that folder into the results table.

Two numbers, in order of importance:

  wrongly dismissed   real problems the agent marked "explained". Must be zero.
  decoys cleared      decoys the agent correctly marked "explained". Higher is better.

"needs_human" is never an error; it is the safe answer. But an agent that sends everything
to a human is useless, so the share sent to review is reported too.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from evals.run_eval import load_truth  # noqa: E402
from make_demo import DATASET_VERSION  # noqa: E402
from reconciler import llm  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.agent import AGENT_VERSION, Investigation, investigate_all  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.checks import run_all  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.ingest import load_matter  # noqa: E402


def score_investigations(invs: list[Investigation], truth: dict[tuple[str, str], str]) -> dict:
    real = [i for i in invs if truth.get((i.type, i.key)) == "real"]
    decoy = [i for i in invs if truth.get((i.type, i.key)) == "decoy"]
    dismissed = [(i.type, i.key) for i in real if i.verdict == "explained"]
    cleared = [(i.type, i.key) for i in decoy if i.verdict == "explained"]
    kept = [i for i in invs if i.verdict != "explained"]
    return {
        "real": len(real), "decoys": len(decoy),
        "wrongly_dismissed": dismissed,
        "decoys_cleared": cleared,
        "decoys_not_cleared": [(i.type, i.key, i.verdict) for i in decoy if i.verdict != "explained"],
        "sent_to_human": sum(i.verdict == "needs_human" for i in invs),
        "overridden_by_guardrail": [(i.type, i.key, i.note) for i in invs if i.note],
        "precision_after": (sum(truth.get((i.type, i.key)) == "real" for i in kept) / len(kept)) if kept else 1.0,
    }


def print_score(r: dict) -> None:
    print(f"wrongly dismissed: {len(r['wrongly_dismissed'])} of {r['real']} real problems")
    for t, k in r["wrongly_dismissed"]:
        print(f"    {t} / {k}")
    print(f"decoys cleared:    {len(r['decoys_cleared'])} of {r['decoys']}")
    for t, k, v in r["decoys_not_cleared"]:
        print(f"    not cleared: {t} / {k} ({v})")
    print(f"sent to a human:   {r['sent_to_human']}")
    print(f"precision of what remains: {r['precision_after']:.0%}")
    for t, k, note in r["overridden_by_guardrail"]:
        print(f"    guardrail: {t} / {k}: {note}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--runs", type=int, default=3, help="agreeing runs needed to clear a finding")
    ap.add_argument("--no-verify", action="store_true", help="skip the second reviewer")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if not llm.configured():
        sys.exit("set RECONCILER_LLM_BASE_URL and RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL first (see README)")

    model = os.environ["RECONCILER_LLM_MODEL"]
    reviewer = llm.reviewer_client() if not args.no_verify else None
    config = {"agent": AGENT_VERSION, "dataset": DATASET_VERSION, "runs": args.runs, "verify": not args.no_verify,
              "reviewer": reviewer.model if reviewer else "same model"}
    print(f"model: {model}   agent: {config}\n")
    matter = load_matter(ROOT / "demo_matter")
    invs = investigate_all(run_all(matter), matter, llm.Client(), runs=args.runs, verify=not args.no_verify, reviewer=reviewer,
                           progress=lambda f, i: print(f"[{i.verdict:<11}] {f.type} / {f.key}"))
    result = score_investigations(invs, load_truth())

    # One file per run, so a later run never overwrites the evidence for an earlier one.
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    runs_dir = ROOT / "evals" / "runs"
    runs_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    out = runs_dir / f"{re.sub(r'[^A-Za-z0-9.]+', '-', model)}_{stamp}.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps({"model": model, "run_at": stamp, "config": config, "score": result,
                               "investigations": [i.to_dict() for i in invs]}, indent=2))
    print()
    print_score(result)
    print(f"\nsaved {out.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    sys.exit(1 if result["wrongly_dismissed"] else 0)

==> ./evals/__init__.py <==

==> ./evals/run_eval.py <==
"""Stage 1: score the first-pass checks against the labelled demo matter.

    python evals/run_eval.py

The first pass is meant to over-flag: it should raise every real problem AND every decoy,
because it has no way to tell them apart. So:

  coverage   of everything it should raise (real + decoy), how much it raised
  unexpected findings that are in neither list (must be none)
  precision  of what it raised, the share that is a real problem. This is the number the
             investigator (stage 2, run_agent_eval.py) exists to improve.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from reconciler.checks import run_all  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.ingest import load_matter  # noqa: E402

TRUTH = ROOT / "evals" / "ground_truth.json"


def load_truth(path: Path = TRUTH) -> dict[tuple[str, str], str]:
    return {(t["type"], t["key"]): t["label"] for t in json.loads(path.read_text())}


def score(matter_dir: Path, truth_path: Path = TRUTH) -> dict:
    truth = load_truth(truth_path)
    found = {(f.type, f.key) for f in run_all(load_matter(matter_dir))}
    real = {k for k, v in truth.items() if v == "real"}
    return {
        "expected": len(truth), "raised": len(found),
        "coverage": len(found & set(truth)) / len(truth),
        "precision": len(found & real) / len(found) if found else 1.0,
        "missed": sorted(set(truth) - found), "unexpected": sorted(found - set(truth)),
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    r = score(ROOT / "demo_matter")
    print(f"expected {r['expected']}  raised {r['raised']}")
    print(f"coverage {r['coverage']:.0%}  first-pass precision {r['precision']:.0%}")
    for label in ("missed", "unexpected"):
        for t, k in r[label]:
            print(f"  {label}: {t} / {k}")
    sys.exit(0 if not r["missed"] and not r["unexpected"] else 1)

==> ./GUIDE.md <==
# Estate Reconciler: a one-page guide for lawyers

## What it is for

You act for a beneficiary. The executor has produced a statement of accounts, bank
statements and a run of letters. Before you can ask useful questions, someone has to
check each against the others. This tool does that first pass and hands you a list of
discrepancies, each with the passages it relies on.

It does not decide whether anyone did anything wrong, and it does not replace your
review. It tells you where to look.

## What you give it

A folder containing the accounts, the bank record and the correspondence. Set up as
recommended, with a model running on your own computer, no document leaves it.

## What you get back

**A lawyer's report.** A dollar total, a table by category, and one entry per
discrepancy, most serious first. Each entry shows what two documents say, where they
differ, the amount, and the exact lines quoted from each.

**A client summary.** The same items, with the same numbering, in plain language and
without citations. It is a draft for you to review and edit before it goes to the client.

**A schedule.** The items as a spreadsheet (CSV), one row each, for your own working papers.

**A verdict on each entry.** Before an item reaches you, the tool looks through the file
for an innocent explanation.

| Verdict | What it means | What you do |
|---|---|---|
| Stands | It looked and found no explanation in the file. | Raise it. |
| Needs a lawyer's review | It found something partial, or the question is one of judgment. | Read the note and decide. |
| Explained by the file | The file itself accounts for it, for example one invoice paid by two cheques. | Spot-check the cited lines, then set it aside. |

**A draft request letter.** It lists every item that was not explained, with its source.
It is a draft. Nothing is ever sent for you.

## How to read an entry

1. Read the title. It states the discrepancy in one line.
2. Open the quoted passages in the original documents and confirm they say what the
   report says they say. Do this for every item you intend to rely on.
3. Read the verdict and its note. Where the tool overrode itself, the note says why.
4. Decide. The tool's verdict is a recommendation.

## How to read the dollar figure

The "amount in question" is the sum of the individual discrepancies that are still open.

- **Nothing is counted twice.** A total that does not add up, or a balance that does not
  match the bank, is a symptom of the items behind it. Those amounts are shown but not added.
- **Explained items are left out.** Once the tool has investigated, anything the file
  itself accounts for is set aside.
- **It is not a damages figure.** It tells you how much money the open questions touch,
  not what was lost or what is owed. Say so when you give the number to a client.

The balance bridge in the lawyer's report shows how the individual items add up to the
gap between the balance the accounts state and the balance at the bank. If the
"unexplained residual" is not zero, something moved the balance that the tool has not
identified, and that is worth a question of its own.

## What it checks

- Every accounts entry against the bank record, and every bank transaction against the accounts
- Whether the accounts add up and the stated balance matches the bank
- Documents a letter says are enclosed or coming that are not in the file
- Letters that describe a document as outstanding after the date on the document
- Figures quoted in letters against the records
- Executor compensation against a starting-point guideline you can set
- Gifts of money in the will against the payments in the accounts
- Penalties and late charges paid out of the estate
- Lawyers' invoices paid by the estate: whether they add up, entries billed twice, and
  time that looks like advice to the executor personally
- Letters against the lawyers' own time records
- Forms dated well before they were signed, and the gap between the final tax assessment
  and the clearance certificate application
- Fees paid to an agent (such as a trust company) alongside full executor compensation
- Tax payments on a reassessment against the notices actually produced

## How cautious it is

The tool is built so that its mistakes fall on the safe side.

- It will sometimes send you an item that turns out to be fine. That costs you a few minutes.
- It is designed not to clear an item that is a real problem. An item is marked
  "explained" only if three separate passes agree, an independent check confirms it, and
  the explanation cites specific lines in the file.
- A total that does not add up is never marked "explained", whatever else is in the file.
- Questions of judgment, such as whether compensation is reasonable, always come to you.

## What it cannot do yet

- Read scanned or photographed statements. It needs text or spreadsheet exports.
- Recognise a promise that is worded loosely ("you should have the appraisal shortly").
- Handle more than one bank account in a single pass.
- Tell you what the law requires. The compensation guideline is a rule of thumb, not a ruling.

## Three habits that keep it reliable

1. Check the citation before you repeat the claim.
2. Treat "explained" as "probably fine", not "confirmed".
3. If an item you expected is missing from the report, do not assume it was cleared. Check
   it yourself; the tool only sees what is in the folder.

==> ./tests/test_report.py <==
"""Tests for the dollar totals, the balance bridge, the catalog and the written reports."""
import csv
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from evals.run_eval import load_truth  # noqa: E402
from make_demo import build  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.agent import Investigation  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.catalog import CATALOG  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.checks import run_all  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.ingest import load_matter  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.models import Evidence  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.report import load_investigations, write_all, write_json  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.summary import balance_bridge, totals  # noqa: E402

# The planted, counted, real amounts other than compensation (which is computed from a guideline):
# funeral 2,000 + roof 8,450 + e-transfer 5,000 + sale figure 13,500 + library gift 10,000 + penalty 350
# + legal fees: invoice shortfall 450 + duplicate entry 225 + personal advice 900
# + tax payment on a reassessment with no notice 1,480. (Agent fees overlap with compensation: not added.)
FIXED_REAL = Decimal("42355.00")
DECOYS = Decimal("6550.00")   # 1,800 + 1,800 + 1,250 + 1,250 + credited duplicate entry 450


class Reports(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        build(cls.tmp.name)
        cls.matter = load_matter(cls.tmp.name)
        cls.findings = run_all(cls.matter)
        truth = load_truth()
        cls.comp = next(f for f in cls.findings if f.type == "compensation_review").amount
        # a perfect investigation: every decoy explained, every real problem stands
        cls.invs = [Investigation(f.type, f.key, "explained" if truth[(f.type, f.key)] == "decoy" else "stands",
                                  "because", [Evidence("bank_statement.csv", "line 10", "x")])
                    for f in cls.findings]

    def test_every_type_raised_is_in_the_catalog(self):
        self.assertEqual({f.type for f in self.findings} - set(CATALOG), set())

    def test_every_finding_has_a_plain_sentence(self):
        self.assertEqual([f.title for f in self.findings if not f.plain], [])

    def test_total_before_investigation_includes_the_decoys(self):
        t = totals(self.findings)
        self.assertFalse(t["investigated"])
        self.assertEqual(t["amount_in_question"], FIXED_REAL + self.comp + DECOYS)

    def test_total_after_investigation_leaves_out_explained_items(self):
        t = totals(self.findings, self.invs)
        self.assertEqual(t["amount_in_question"], FIXED_REAL + self.comp)
        self.assertEqual(t["by_status"]["explained"]["count"], 6)
        self.assertEqual(t["open_count"], 21)

    def test_symptoms_are_never_added_to_the_total(self):
        """The $5,000 total error and the balance gap overlap with the items behind them."""
        without = [f for f in self.findings if f.type not in ("arithmetic_error", "balance_mismatch", "date_mismatch")]
        self.assertEqual(totals(self.findings)["amount_in_question"], totals(without)["amount_in_question"])

    def test_reprint_date_is_never_read_as_the_issue_date(self):
        notice = next(d for d in self.matter.texts if d.doc_type == "notice_of_assessment")
        self.assertEqual((str(notice.date), str(notice.reprinted)), ("2024-12-17", "2025-07-02"))
        timeline = next(f for f in self.findings if f.type == "timeline_contradiction")
        self.assertEqual(len([e for e in timeline.evidence if e.doc.startswith("correspondence/")]), 2)
        self.assertIn("reprinted", timeline.detail)

    def test_reassessment_payment_with_its_notice_is_not_raised(self):
        flagged = {f.key for f in self.findings if f.type == "unsupported_tax_payment"}
        self.assertEqual(flagged, {"D-17"})       # D-18 has its notice in the file

    def test_agent_fees_are_shown_but_not_added_twice(self):
        offset = next(f for f in self.findings if f.type == "agent_fee_offset")
        self.assertEqual(offset.amount, Decimal("7200.00"))
        without = [f for f in self.findings if f.type != "agent_fee_offset"]
        self.assertEqual(totals(self.findings)["amount_in_question"], totals(without)["amount_in_question"])

    def test_balance_bridge_traces_the_whole_gap(self):
        b = balance_bridge(self.matter, self.findings)
        self.assertEqual(b["residual"], Decimal("0.00"))
        self.assertNotEqual(b["gap"], Decimal(0))

    def test_balance_bridge_shows_a_residual_when_an_item_is_unidentified(self):
        fewer = [f for f in self.findings if f.type != "undisclosed_transaction" or f.key != "2023-10-20:5000.00"]
        self.assertEqual(balance_bridge(self.matter, fewer)["residual"], Decimal("-5000.00"))

    def test_clean_file_has_no_gap_and_nothing_in_question(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            build(tmp, seeded=False, decoys=False)
            m = load_matter(tmp)
            self.assertEqual(totals(run_all(m))["amount_in_question"], Decimal(0))
            self.assertEqual(balance_bridge(m, [])["residual"], Decimal("0.00"))

    def test_reports_are_written_and_agree_with_each_other(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as out:
            out = Path(out)
            write_json(self.invs, out / "investigations.json")
            invs = load_investigations(out / "investigations.json")      # round trip
            write_all(self.matter, self.findings, invs, out, "Estate of M. Hartwell")
            lawyer = (out / "report_lawyer.html").read_text()
            client = (out / "report_client.html").read_text()
            total = f"${FIXED_REAL + self.comp:,.2f}"
            self.assertIn(total, lawyer)
            self.assertIn(total, client)
            self.assertIn("Draft for your lawyer to review", client)
            self.assertNotIn("row D-", client)                # no citations or entry ids in the client summary
            self.assertIn("Unexplained residual", lawyer)
            rows = list(csv.DictReader((out / "schedule.csv").open()))
            self.assertEqual(len(rows), len(self.findings))
            added = sum(Decimal(r["amount"]) for r in rows if r["added_to_total"] == "yes")
            self.assertEqual(added, FIXED_REAL + self.comp)
            self.assertNotIn("D-11", (out / "draft_request_letter.md").read_text())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

==> ./tests/test_reconciler.py <==
"""python -m unittest discover tests"""
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from evals.run_eval import score  # noqa: E402
from make_demo import build  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.checks import run_all  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.ingest import load_matter  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.retrieval import ask  # noqa: E402


class SeededMatter(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        build(cls.tmp.name)
        cls.matter = load_matter(cls.tmp.name)
        cls.findings = run_all(cls.matter)

    def test_raises_every_planted_item_and_nothing_else(self):
        r = score(Path(self.tmp.name))
        self.assertEqual(r["missed"], [])
        self.assertEqual(r["unexpected"], [])

    def test_every_finding_cites_a_real_location(self):
        for f in self.findings:
            self.assertTrue(f.evidence, f.title)
            for ev in f.evidence:
                self.assertTrue((Path(self.tmp.name) / ev.doc).exists(), ev.doc)

    def test_retrieval_surfaces_the_missing_tax_documents(self):
        hits = ask(self.matter, self.findings, "Were the T3 trust returns ever provided?")["passages"]
        self.assertTrue(any("T3" in c.text for c in hits[:3]))


class CleanMatter(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_consistent_file_raises_nothing(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            build(tmp, seeded=False, decoys=False)
            self.assertEqual([f.title for f in run_all(load_matter(tmp))], [])

    def test_decoys_alone_raise_only_decoys(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            build(tmp, seeded=False, decoys=True)
            keys = {(f.type, f.key) for f in run_all(load_matter(tmp))}
            self.assertEqual(keys, {
                ("amount_mismatch", "D-11"), ("undisclosed_transaction", "2023-08-28:1800.00"),
                ("undisclosed_transaction", "2023-09-05:1250.00"),
                ("undisclosed_transaction", "2023-09-07:1250.00"), ("missing_document", "inventory"),
                ("duplicate_time_entry", "2023-07-11:JW:450.00")})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

==> ./tests/test_agent.py <==
"""Tests for the investigator's plumbing and guardrails, using a scripted stand-in for the model.

These prove that the loop, the tools and the overrides behave. They say nothing about how
well a real model investigates; evals/run_agent_eval.py measures that.
"""
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from evals.run_agent_eval import score_investigations  # noqa: E402
from evals.run_eval import load_truth  # noqa: E402
from make_demo import build  # noqa: E402
from evals.make_table import build_table  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.agent import investigate, investigate_all, investigate_checked  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.checks import run_all  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.ingest import load_matter  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.letter import draft_request  # noqa: E402
from reconciler.tools import Toolbox  # noqa: E402


def call(name, **args):
    return {"role": "assistant", "content": None,
            "tool_calls": [{"id": "c1", "type": "function",
                            "function": {"name": name, "arguments": json.dumps(args)}}]}


class Scripted:
    """Plays back a fixed list of assistant messages; records what it was sent."""
    def __init__(self, *turns):
        self.turns, self.seen, self.opts = list(turns), [], []

    def complete(self, messages, tools=None, **opts):
        self.seen.append(list(messages))
        self.opts.append(opts)
        if not self.turns:
            return {"role": "assistant", "content": "I am not sure."}
        turn = self.turns.pop(0)
        return turn(messages) if callable(turn) else turn


# The two Brightway cheques (the split-payment decoy). Line numbers shift when the demo gains
# earlier bank lines; test_tools_can_reach_every_decoy_explanation fails first if they drift.
BANK_14 = "bank_statement.csv :: line 12"
BANK_28 = "bank_statement.csv :: line 13"


class AgentGuardrails(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        build(cls.tmp.name)
        cls.matter = load_matter(cls.tmp.name)
        cls.findings = run_all(cls.matter)
        cls.by_key = {(f.type, f.key): f for f in cls.findings}
        cls.split = cls.by_key[("amount_mismatch", "D-11")]

    def test_tools_can_reach_every_decoy_explanation(self):
        """The eval is only fair if the explanations are findable with the tools given."""
        box = Toolbox(self.matter)
        cheques = box.call("find_transactions", {"source": "bank", "text": "brightway"})
        self.assertEqual([c["ref"] for c in cheques], [BANK_14, BANK_28])
        self.assertEqual(box.call("add_amounts", {"amounts": [1800, 1800]}), {"total": "3600.00"})
        wires = box.call("find_transactions", {"source": "bank", "text": "77812"})
        self.assertEqual({w["direction"] for w in wires}, {"money in", "money out"})
        credit = box.call("search_documents", {"query": "courtesy credit entry recorded twice"})
        self.assertIn("-450.00", credit[0]["text"])
        hits = box.call("search_documents", {"query": "assets of the estate owned at death"})
        self.assertTrue(any(h["ref"].startswith("schedule_a_assets.txt") for h in hits))

    def test_explained_with_new_valid_citations_is_accepted(self):
        client = Scripted(
            call("find_transactions", source="bank", text="brightway"),
            call("add_amounts", amounts=[1800, 1800]),
            call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained", explanation="Two cheques of $1,800 total $3,600.",
                 citations=[BANK_14, BANK_28]))
        inv = investigate(self.split, self.matter, client)
        self.assertEqual((inv.verdict, inv.note), ("explained", ""))
        self.assertEqual(len(inv.citations), 2)
        self.assertEqual([t["tool"] for t in inv.trace], ["find_transactions", "add_amounts"])
        # the tool result was fed back to the model
        self.assertEqual(client.seen[1][-1]["role"], "tool")

    def test_invented_citation_is_overridden(self):
        client = Scripted(call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained", explanation="See the invoice.",
                               citations=["invoices/brightway.pdf :: page 1"]))
        inv = investigate(self.split, self.matter, client)
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "needs_human")
        self.assertEqual(inv.rejected_citations, ["invoices/brightway.pdf :: page 1"])

    def test_explained_without_new_evidence_is_overridden(self):
        original = f"{self.split.evidence[0].doc} :: {self.split.evidence[0].locator}"
        client = Scripted(call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained", explanation="Probably fine.",
                               citations=[original]))
        self.assertEqual(investigate(self.split, self.matter, client).verdict, "needs_human")

    def test_same_sized_second_problem_cannot_clear_an_arithmetic_error(self):
        """Regression: a local model cleared the $5,000 total error by citing the $5,000 e-transfer."""
        for kind, key, expect in (("arithmetic_error", "total_disbursements", "stands"),
                                  ("balance_mismatch", "balance_on_hand", "needs_human")):
            client = Scripted(
                call("find_transactions", source="bank", min_amount=5000, max_amount=5000),
                lambda msgs: call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained",
                                  explanation="The $5,000 e-transfer accounts for the difference.",
                                  citations=[json.loads(msgs[-1]["content"])[0]["ref"]]))
            inv = investigate(self.by_key[(kind, key)], self.matter, client)
            self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, expect)
            self.assertEqual(len(inv.citations), 1)      # the lead is kept
            self.assertIn("lead", inv.note)

    def test_stands_needs_no_citation(self):
        client = Scripted(call("submit_verdict", verdict="stands", explanation="Nothing found.", citations=[]))
        self.assertEqual(investigate(self.split, self.matter, client).verdict, "stands")

    def test_never_submitting_becomes_needs_human(self):
        looping = [call("search_documents", query="brightway")] * 20
        inv = investigate(self.split, self.matter, Scripted(*looping), max_steps=4)
        self.assertEqual((inv.verdict, len(inv.trace)), ("needs_human", 4))

    def test_model_is_warned_before_its_last_step(self):
        looping = [call("search_documents", query="brightway")] * 3
        client = Scripted(*looping, call("submit_verdict", verdict="stands", explanation="x", citations=[]))
        inv = investigate(self.split, self.matter, client, max_steps=4)
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "stands")
        self.assertIn("last step", client.seen[-1][-1]["content"])
        self.assertNotIn("last step", client.seen[-2][-1]["content"])

    def test_plain_text_reply_is_nudged_once_then_needs_human(self):
        client = Scripted()
        inv = investigate(self.split, self.matter, client)
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "needs_human")
        self.assertEqual(len(client.seen), 2)

    def test_bad_tool_arguments_do_not_crash(self):
        client = Scripted(
            call("find_transactions", source="ledger", date_from="last August"),
            call("no_such_tool"),
            call("submit_verdict", verdict="stands", explanation="x", citations=[]))
        inv = investigate(self.split, self.matter, client)
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "stands")
        self.assertTrue(all("error" in t["result"] for t in inv.trace))

    def test_model_error_becomes_needs_human(self):
        class Broken:
            def complete(self, *a, **k):
                raise ConnectionError("refused")
        self.assertEqual(investigate(self.split, self.matter, Broken()).verdict, "needs_human")

    def test_judgment_findings_never_reach_the_model(self):
        client = Scripted()
        inv = investigate(self.by_key[("compensation_review", "D-09")], self.matter, client)
        self.assertEqual((inv.verdict, client.seen), ("needs_human", []))

    def test_scoring_and_letter_end_to_end(self):
        """A stand-in that clears the split payment correctly and wrongly dismisses one real problem."""
        def policy(messages):
            brief = messages[1]["content"]
            if "D-11" in brief:
                return call("find_transactions", source="bank", text="brightway")
            if "D-04" in brief:   # the planted, real, unsupported roof repair
                return call("search_documents", query="alder lane property")
            return call("submit_verdict", verdict="stands", explanation="Nothing found.", citations=[])

        def finish(messages):
            brief = messages[1]["content"]
            refs = [r["ref"] for r in json.loads(messages[-1]["content"])]
            new = [r for r in refs if r not in brief]
            return call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained", explanation="Found it.", citations=new[:2])

        class Policy:
            def complete(self, messages, tools=None, **opts):
                return finish(messages) if messages[-1]["role"] == "tool" else policy(messages)

        invs = investigate_all(self.findings, self.matter, Policy(), runs=1, verify=False)
        r = score_investigations(invs, load_truth())
        self.assertEqual(r["wrongly_dismissed"], [("unsupported_entry", "D-04")])
        self.assertEqual(r["decoys_cleared"], [("amount_mismatch", "D-11")])
        letter = draft_request(self.findings, invs)
        self.assertIn("NOT SENT", letter)
        self.assertNotIn("D-11", letter)          # explained items are left out of the letter
        self.assertIn("T3 trust return", letter)  # standing items are in it


FIND = call("find_transactions", source="bank", text="brightway")
CLEAR = call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained", explanation="Two cheques of $1,800 total $3,600.",
             citations=[BANK_14, BANK_28])
KEEP = call("submit_verdict", verdict="stands", explanation="Nothing found.", citations=[])


def review(decision, reason="ok"):
    return call("submit_review", decision=decision, reason=reason)


class ClearingTakesAgreement(AgentGuardrails):
    """Clearing a finding needs every repeat run to agree, then a second reviewer."""

    def run_checked(self, *turns, **kw):
        client = Scripted(*turns)
        return investigate_checked(self.split, self.matter, client, self.findings, **kw), client

    def test_three_agreeing_runs_and_a_confirming_reviewer_clear_it(self):
        inv, client = self.run_checked(FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, review("confirm"))
        self.assertEqual((inv.verdict, inv.note), ("explained", ""))
        self.assertEqual(inv.checks["runs"], ["explained"] * 3)
        self.assertTrue(inv.checks["review"]["confirmed"])
        # the first run is deterministic; repeats are sampled with a fixed seed
        self.assertEqual(client.opts[0], {})
        self.assertEqual(client.opts[2], {"temperature": 0.5, "seed": 1})

    def test_one_dissenting_run_sends_it_to_a_lawyer(self):
        inv, client = self.run_checked(FIND, CLEAR, FIND, KEEP)
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "needs_human")
        self.assertIn("disagreed", inv.note)
        self.assertEqual(len(inv.citations), 2)        # the first run's account is kept as a lead
        self.assertEqual(len(client.seen), 4)          # stopped at the first dissent; no reviewer call

    def test_reviewer_rejection_sends_it_to_a_lawyer(self):
        inv, _ = self.run_checked(FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, review("reject", "only half the amount"))
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "needs_human")
        self.assertIn("only half the amount", inv.note)

    def test_reviewer_that_gives_no_answer_counts_as_rejection(self):
        inv, _ = self.run_checked(FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR)   # reviewer replies in plain text
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "needs_human")

    def test_reviewer_sees_only_the_claim_and_is_told_which_citations_are_in_dispute(self):
        _, client = self.run_checked(FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, review("confirm"))
        system, user = client.seen[-1]
        self.assertIn("second reviewer", system["content"])
        packet = json.loads(user["content"])
        by_ref = {c["ref"]: c["also_flagged_as"] for c in packet["cited_evidence"]}
        self.assertEqual(by_ref[BANK_14], [])                       # only this finding relies on it
        self.assertTrue(any("2023-08-28" in t for t in by_ref[BANK_28]))   # the other half is flagged too

    def test_a_separate_reviewer_model_gets_only_the_review(self):
        investigator = Scripted(FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR, FIND, CLEAR)
        reviewer = Scripted(review("reject", "not persuaded"))
        inv = investigate_checked(self.split, self.matter, investigator, self.findings, reviewer=reviewer)
        self.assertEqual((len(investigator.seen), len(reviewer.seen)), (6, 1))
        self.assertEqual(inv.verdict, "needs_human")

    def test_invoice_arithmetic_and_personal_fees_cannot_be_cleared_by_a_model(self):
        arith = self.by_key[("invoice_arithmetic", "invoices/invoice_whitlock_pryce_2023-09.txt")]
        client = Scripted(call("search_documents", query="courtesy credit"),
                          lambda msgs: call("submit_verdict", verdict="explained", explanation="A credit covers it.",
                                            citations=[json.loads(msgs[-1]["content"])[0]["ref"]]))
        self.assertEqual(investigate(arith, self.matter, client).verdict, "stands")
        personal = self.by_key[("personal_legal_fees", "2023-07-25:JW:900.00")]
        untouched = Scripted()
        self.assertEqual((investigate(personal, self.matter, untouched).verdict, untouched.seen), ("needs_human", []))

    def test_agent_fee_offset_never_reaches_the_model(self):
        untouched = Scripted()
        inv = investigate(self.by_key[("agent_fee_offset", "D-09")], self.matter, untouched)
        self.assertEqual((inv.verdict, untouched.seen), ("needs_human", []))

    def test_a_first_run_that_keeps_the_finding_is_not_repeated(self):
        inv, client = self.run_checked(KEEP)
        self.assertEqual((inv.verdict, len(client.seen), inv.checks), ("stands", 1, {}))


class ResultsTable(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_table_is_built_from_run_files_and_shows_the_worst_case(self):
        score = {"real": 11, "decoys": 5, "wrongly_dismissed": [], "decoys_cleared": [1] * 5, "sent_to_human": 2}
        bad = dict(score, wrongly_dismissed=[["arithmetic_error", "total_disbursements"]], decoys_cleared=[1] * 3)
        cfg = {"agent": "v4", "runs": 3, "verify": True}
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            for n, s in enumerate((score, bad)):
                (Path(tmp) / f"m_{n}.json").write_text(json.dumps({"model": "m", "config": cfg, "score": s}))
            (Path(tmp) / "old.json").write_text("[]")              # an old-format file is skipped
            table = build_table(Path(tmp))
        self.assertIn("| `m` | v4, demo set 1, 3 run(s) to clear, second reviewer | 2 | 1 of 11 | 3 to 5 of 5 | 2 |", table)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
